# QA review log

Findings from three sources, all in the same bullet format: the automated suite (`## QA auto <date> (commit <sha>)`, four times a day, ids like `[auto:flow:house-manual:H02-draw-plan]`), the QA judge routine (`## QA judge <date> (commit <sha>)`, verdict, Product gap and UI findings, ids like `[judge:hm-items-cards]`), and the Antigravity nightly tester when it runs (`## QA <date> (commit <sha>)`). Plus the builder's own visual reviews. Each finding is one bullet:

```
- [ ] [Critical|Major|Minor|Product gap|UI] <problem> - steps: <how to reproduce> - expected: <SPEC AC / ui-standards rule / PRODUCT.md step> - actual: <what happened> - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/<file>.png
```

Severity: **Critical** = crash, data loss, a core flow cannot be completed, or a screen the user cannot leave. **Major** = a feature misses its acceptance criterion. **Product gap** = the feature exists but a homeowner or contractor would not accept it as described in PRODUCT.md; the builder treats it like Major. **UI** = a numbered rule in docs/ui-standards.md fails (Major for rules 1–6, 13, 15, 16, 28; otherwise Minor). **Minor** = polish.

The builder fixes findings (Critical, Major and Product gap before starting new tasks) and marks them:

```
- [x] [Major] ... - fixed in <sha>
```

Findings that are really spec changes go to BLOCKERS.md under "Decisions to confirm" instead.

Builder visual reviews (V0, V1, ...) go under `## Builder visual review V<n> (commit <sha>)` with the rules checked and what was changed.

---

## QA 2026-10-19 (commit d79790d)

- **Verdict (House Manual)**: Right now it is only a five-tab navigation shell where every tab says "Nothing here yet. This screen is on its way." with a dummy detail button. The biggest gap is that there is no home setup or house model yet—you cannot name a house, draw a floor plan, or add a single item.
- **Verdict (Field Log)**: Like House Manual, it is a bare five-tab scaffold with placeholder text on Today, Jobs, Clock, Schedule and More. The biggest gap is that a contractor cannot sign in, create a company or job, or clock in.

### Findings

- [x] [UI] Major: Landscape phone width (≥ 600 dp, e.g. 891 dp on Pixel 8 landscape) keeps a bottom navigation bar instead of switching to a navigation rail - steps: Launch either app on `qa_pixel` (1080×2400, 420 dpi) and rotate to landscape (`2400×1080`, width ~891 dp) - expected: AC-U1 / `docs/ui-standards.md` rule 1 ("On width ≥ 600 dp use a navigation rail") - actual: `NavigationSuiteScaffold` default checks compact height first and renders a wide bottom navigation bar in phone landscape; it only switches to a rail when both width and height are ≥ 600 dp (tablet `1800×2400`) - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-schedule-landscape-rail.png - fixed in 77955a2
- [ ] [UI] Minor: At 200% font scale, bottom navigation bar labels wrap mid-word across two lines ("Remin / ders" in House Manual, "Sched / ule" in Field Log) - steps: Set `adb shell settings put system font_scale 2.0` and open House Manual or Field Log in portrait - expected: AC-U9 / `docs/ui-standards.md` rule 32 ("200% font scale clips nothing; rows grow") without broken mid-word label wrapping in the bottom navigation bar - actual: "Reminders" wraps as "Remin\nders" and "Schedule" wraps as "Sched\nule" - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/hm-reminders-font200.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: `:core-ui` has committed `@PreviewTest` screenshot tests for only 4 of the 6 component preview groups (`LinkChipRowPreview`, `FormFieldsPreview`, and the app shells have no `@PreviewTest` reference PNGs yet) - steps: Inspect `core-ui/src/screenshotTest/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/coreui/ComponentScreenshots.kt` after T005 - expected: AC-U12 / `docs/ui-standards.md` rule 36 and PLAN.md T005 ("reference PNGs committed for the shells and components") - actual: Only `RecordListItemScreenshot`, `EmptyStateScreenshot`, `LoadingStateScreenshot`, and `ErrorStateScreenshot` exist (12 PNGs total) - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/hm-home.png

## QA judge 2026-10-20 (commit cbf81fa)

Judged the results the suite published for cbf81fa (run 39419820023, the first run of qa.yml). `build` HEAD is already two commits ahead (3fd544b Q001, 65ec903 the auto entry); the suite has not tested HEAD. There are no screenshots to judge: all three emulator jobs failed before the first flow ran, so `results.json` has `stages: []` and `latest/` holds only JVM, lint, backend and coverage output. The UI audit (ui-standards rules 1-36), the committed-PNG comparison and the walkthrough scoring could not be done this run; the builder's own reference PNGs under `*/src/screenshotTest*/reference/` are the only pictures of the apps that exist and they are not evidence of what runs on a device.

Verdict: House Manual: nobody has seen this build on a device; the QA pipeline that was supposed to be Nate's eyes died at emulator boot, so M0 and the plan editor (T011-T015) are still only unit-tested. The single biggest gap is that the device suite does not run, and until it does every ticked task is unverified against PRODUCT.md. / Field Log: same story; sign-in, company and team (T008-T009) have JVM tests and 51 backend tests but no on-device check, and the walkthrough cannot start at F1. Fix the suite before building more surface area.

- [x] [Major] QA device jobs never boot the emulator: `emulator` exits at once with `FATAL | Not enough space to create userdata partition. Available: 6236 MB at /home/runner/.android/avd/test.avd, need 7372.80 MB`, then android-emulator-runner polls adb for 600 s and fails with "Timeout waiting for emulator to boot" [judge:qa-emulator-disk] - steps: open run 39419820023, jobs "Device suite (house-manual)" step "Create AVD snapshot" (12:28:56 UTC), "Device suite (field-log)" and "Handoff between the apps" (12:42:13 UTC); all three carry the same FATAL line - expected: qa.yml runs the device suite per app plus handoff and publishes screenshots (CLAUDE.md "What each file is for", qa/README.md) - actual: 0 device stages, no screenshots, `qa-out/device/*` empty. Cause: qa.yml installs a second SDK under `${{ github.workspace }}/.android-sdk` (`ANDROID_HOME`, line 32) on the same filesystem as `~/.android/avd`, leaving ~6.2 GB, while emulator 37.2.12 needs a 7.2 GB userdata partition for the pixel_7 / API 36 image. Fix in qa.yml (builder, not app code): set `disk-size` on the three `reactivecircus/android-emulator-runner@v2` steps (e.g. `disk-size: 4096M`) and/or free runner disk before the emulator steps (`/usr/share/dotnet`, `/opt/ghc`, `/usr/local/.ghcup`, CodeQL) or use the runner's preinstalled SDK for the device jobs. Also: the AVD cache key `avd-36-google_apis-x86_64-pixel7-v1` missed in all three jobs; check that the cache save step runs after a successful boot. - screenshot: none (no device output on qa-results:latest/) - fixed: disk-size 3072M on all three emulator steps in qa.yml
- [x] [Major] The suite reports a run with zero device stages as a clean run: summary.md says "Device stages: 0/0 passed", the auto entry says "Tested: 0 device stages ... Passed: 0. New issues: 13" and files nothing about the three failed jobs, and `qa/last-tested.txt` was advanced to cbf81fa as if it had been tested [judge:qa-silent-device-failure] - steps: compare `git show origin/qa-results:latest/results.json` (`"stages": []`) with the "## QA auto 2026-10-20 (commit cbf81fa)" entry above and with the run's job conclusions (3 failures) - expected: a device job that produces no results is itself a Critical finding (the definition of done keys on "QA has tested HEAD" and on `qa/last-tested.txt`, so an empty suite must not count as a test) - actual: no finding, last-tested advanced. Fix in qa/scripts/summarize.py and publish-results.sh: when any device job's output directory is missing or `stages` is empty, file `[Critical] device suite produced no results for <app>` with the job's failure step, and do not write `qa/last-tested.txt`. - screenshot: none - fixed in the next commit (summarize.py files [Critical] per missing/empty device job and writes tested-ok only when all of house-manual, field-log, handoff produced stages; qa.yml updates qa/last-tested.txt only then)

## QA judge 2026-10-21 (commit 7823ec2)

Judged the results the suite published for 7823ec2 (run 39493986015, the first scheduled run). `build` HEAD is four commits ahead (a823b55 auto entry, 1e73f82 diagnosis, a6fea0c and 0522771 Q003 parts 2-3); the suite has not tested HEAD. Second run in a row with no screenshots: all three emulator jobs died in "Create AVD snapshot" / "Run the handoff suite" with the same `FATAL | Not enough space to create userdata partition. Available: 6232.53 MB at /home/runner/.android/avd/test.avd, need 7372.80 MB` as cbf81fa, because a scheduled run uses the workflow file on `main`, and `main` was still at 42bfb58 (no `disk-size`) when the device jobs started; the in-run sync job copied the fixed qa.yml to `main` (3f2878b) only after they had been scheduled. The builder's diagnosis in 1e73f82 is right. The UI audit (rules 1-36), the committed-PNG comparison and the walkthrough scoring could not be done; `results.json` has `stages: []` and `latest/` holds only JVM, lint, backend, screenshot-report and coverage output. The next scheduled slot (23:20 UTC) had not fired by 00:50 UTC on 2026-10-21; GitHub runs schedules late under load (run 2 fired 3 h 20 min after its 17:20 slot), so that is an observation, not a finding, until the next judge run.

Verdict: House Manual: still unseen on a device after two suite runs; T011-T015 (onboarding, plan editor, blueprint) exist only as unit tests, 230 green JVM tests and reference PNGs the builder drew for itself. The biggest gap is unchanged: the QA pipeline has never booted an emulator, so nothing ticked is verified against PRODUCT.md, and the builder spent this cycle writing instrumented tests (Q003) that have also never run. / Field Log: same; sign-in, company and team (T008-T009) have 51 backend tests and no on-device check, F1 has never been attempted. If Nate installed either build tonight he would be the first person to see it run.

- [x] [Major] `qa/last-tested.txt` was advanced to 7823ec2 by a run with zero device stages, so the repo now claims a sha was tested that nobody tested [judge:qa-last-tested-false] - steps: `git log --oneline -2 -- qa/last-tested.txt` (a823b55 "QA auto 2026-10-20 21:23 UTC (commit 7823ec2)") against `git show origin/qa-results:latest/results.json` (`"stages": []`) - expected: definition of done item 2 keys on `qa/last-tested.txt`; the builder's own fix (qa.yml line 293, `if [ -f qa-publish/tested-ok ]`) is meant to leave it alone when any device job produced nothing - actual: the run executed the stale qa.yml on `main` (42bfb58 line 289 writes the sha unconditionally), so the gate was bypassed while summarize.py (checked out from `build`) correctly filed the three Critical findings. ## QA 2026-10-21 (commit 8bcf07f)

- **Verdict (House Manual)**: The onboarding flow, floor plan grid editor, and blueprint viewer are solidly functional on device—drawing rooms, editing shapes with corner handles, and toggling utility layers all work smoothly in dark and light modes. The single biggest gap is that the core homeowner features—items, equipment inventory, data plate OCR, and warranty tracking—are completely unbuilt placeholders.
- **Verdict (Field Log)**: The authentication gate, team management with invite code generation/rotation, company settings, and role-gated Today empty states are fully wired and functional on screen. The single biggest gap is that actual contractor job execution—jobs list, time clock punches, photo logs, and daily logs—remains unbuilt scaffolding.

### Findings

- [x] [Critical] Espresso crashes during test idle synchronization on Android API 37: `java.lang.NoSuchMethodException: android.hardware.input.InputManager.getInstance []` - steps: Run `./gradlew connectedDebugAndroidTest` on `qa_pixel` (API 37) - expected: All instrumented tests run and pass on device - actual: All tests in both apps fail during initialization inside `androidx.test.espresso.base.InputManagerEventInjectionStrategy.initialize` because reflection on `InputManager.getInstance` was deprecated/removed in API 36/37 - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-launch.png - fixed in this commit
- [x] [Major] `backend/wrangler.toml` hardcodes `ENVIRONMENT = "production"`, causing dev sign-in email-code flow to fail with 502 Bad Gateway - steps: Start backend with `cd backend && npx wrangler dev` without `.dev.vars`, then submit email on Field Log or House Manual Sign-in screen - expected: In dev mode without a Resend key, the code is logged to console and the endpoint returns `{"ok":true}` (AC-B1) - actual: `backend/src/auth/emailCode.ts` checks `if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY) return env.ENVIRONMENT !== "production"`; because `wrangler.toml` defines `ENVIRONMENT = "production"`, `sendCode` returns `false` and `/auth/email/start` returns 502 `send_failed` - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-signin-typed.png - fixed in this commit
- [ ] [Minor] `RetrofitAuthApi` silently swallows `IOException` without logging to logcat, leaving no debugging trace when network calls fail - steps: Trigger any network error or backend failure while attempting sign-in - expected: Network exceptions or HTTP errors are logged to logcat with tag or stack trace for developer/QA visibility - actual: Catch blocks in `RetrofitAuthApi.kt` convert `IOException` directly to `AuthResult.Offline` without any logging, making connection issues hard to diagnose from device logs - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-signin.png

## QA judge 2026-10-21 (commit f97d6ee)

Judged the results the suite published for f97d6ee (run of 2026-10-21 02:12 UTC, the first run with device stages: 57 stages, 24 passed). `build` HEAD is seven commits ahead (545fbeb Q003, 8bcf07f and 3eb9477 crawler and acceptance fixes, 8bf7eba the Antigravity QA entry, a9aa17d Espresso and wrangler fixes, 301c3da cold start); the suite has not tested HEAD, so several items below are already addressed there and unverified. First run with screenshots: 258 PNGs on `qa-results:latest/` (crawler shots per catalog screen in dark, 200% font and landscape; launch, after-monkey and release-launch shots; no Maestro flow screenshots, see [judge:qa-flow-screenshots-lost]). The crawler renders each screen alone in `QaActivity`, so bottom bars and the app shell are not in any picture; rules 1 and 5 could not be judged from them.

Verdict: House Manual: the house model is real now: onboarding, the grid editor (L-shapes, doors, stairs, overlap warning, undo) and the blueprint read like a product and hold up in dark mode and at 200%. But finish setup and the app drops you on a Home tab that says "Nothing here yet"; the button said "Draw your floor plan" and nothing opened; the editor is two taps away on another tab, and everything past H3 is still a placeholder. / Field Log: on this build a contractor cannot get past the first screen: Send code returned 502 because the local backend ran as `production` without a Resend key (Antigravity found it, a9aa17d fixes it, unverified), so F1 and F2 never ran. Behind the gate Team, Company and the role-gated More are tidy, but Today and More paint their first row under the status bar, and nothing a crew would use (jobs, clock, photos) exists yet.

## QA judge 2026-10-21 (commit 82537dd)

Judged the results the suite published for 82537dd (run of 2026-10-21 10:50 UTC, 57 stages, 23 passed). `build` HEAD is three commits ahead (4105318 room page subtitle and empty state, 6bef85c uninstall before install, a228690 flows on ids); the suite has not tested HEAD. This run is a House Manual run only: the Field Log debug APK did not install (a release-signed Field Log from the previous run's release stage was still on the cached AVD, so `INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE`), the androidTest APK did not install either, and every Field Log stage after that ran against the stale build or nothing. The 50 Field Log crawler PNGs on `latest/` are byte-identical to the previous run's and the 20 Field Log screens inside `house-manual/crawler/` are the same leftovers ([judge:qa-crawler-sdcard-stale]); none of them shows 82537dd. The only picture of Field Log at this sha is `handoff/screenshots/field-log-release-launch.png` (the Sign in screen, correct). House Manual: 84 fresh crawler PNGs (27 catalog screens in dark, 200% and landscape, plus light for onboarding), 16 of 18 instrumented tests green, H01 passes on device for the first time, H02/H02b/H03/H15 fail. Maestro failure screenshots are still not published ([judge:qa-flow-screenshots-lost]), which is exactly what would have settled H02 below.

Verdict: House Manual: onboarding now passes end to end on a device and the editor and blueprint screens hold up in dark, 200% and landscape; the fixes since the last judge (status bar, draw-plan landing, room page) are in or on the way. But the walkthrough still stops at the second step: after Skip → House → Edit plan the editor opens and sits for 17 s with no "Add room" to tap, and nobody can see why because the failure screenshot is thrown away; a room can be deleted with one tap and no way back once you leave the editor. / Field Log: not tested this run at all; the install failed before a single Field Log screen of 82537dd was drawn, and the suite reported monkey "pass" and a tour that was never of this build. The builder's uninstall-first fix (6bef85c) is the right one; until a run passes F1 and F2 with the dev backend, Field Log's status is the same as last time: Team, Company and More look right in old pictures, Send code has never produced a Code field on a device.

- [x] [UI] Major: "Delete room" in the plan editor deletes at once: no `AlertDialog`, no "Deleted · Undo" snackbar; the only recovery is the top-bar undo, which is gone once you leave the editor (autosave) or rotate; the stairs trash icon on the inspector does the same [judge:hm-plan-delete-no-confirm] - steps: House → Edit plan, tap a room, tap "Delete room" (or the trash beside "Stairs to Upper"); compare House tab → long-press a room → Delete, which asks "Delete Kitchen?" with Cancel/Delete - expected: ui-standards rule 16 (destructive actions always confirm; deleting from a list offers Undo in a snackbar); the two delete paths should behave the same - actual: `PlanEditorScreen.kt` line 568 `OutlinedButton(onClick = { onEvent(PlanEditorEvent.Delete) })` and line 639 `DeleteStair` go straight to the ViewModel; `HouseDialogs` has the `ConfirmDelete` dialog the editor lacks - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual/plan-stairs-dark.png - fixed in this commit: confirm dialog for room and stairs delete
- [ ] [UI] Minor: at 200% font scale the blueprint hides three of the four room names (Living room, Dining room, Mudroom; only Kitchen survives), so the plan a homeowner reads at the accessibility size has unnamed rooms [judge:hm-blueprint-names-font200] - steps: `settings put system font_scale 2.0`, open the House tab; or `house-blueprint-font200.png` against `house-blueprint-dark.png` - expected: ui-standards rule 32 (200% clips nothing; rows grow), walkthrough H3 ("room names, dimensions") - actual: `drawBlueprint` drops any label wider than its room instead of shrinking it, wrapping it on two lines or ellipsizing; at 200% the labels no longer fit a 14' × 10' room at the portrait scale - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual/house-blueprint-font200.png

## QA judge 2026-10-21 (commit 3e0cfbc)

Judged the results the suite published for 3e0cfbc (run of 2026-10-21 16:32 UTC, 57 stages, 31 passed). `build` HEAD is three commits ahead (8cf9be8 Q005a dev routes, be2027d new rooms land beside existing ones, 984eef6 per-app crawler dirs); the suite has not tested HEAD, and be2027d is the fix for the biggest item below. First run that publishes Maestro screenshots: the H02 flow's seven step shots and the failure captures finally show what the editor does after Skip → House → Edit plan, and they settle last entry's open question (the editor opens fine; the rooms stack). The Field Log job is unusable again, for a new reason: a "System UI isn't responding" dialog (SystemUI ANR at 16:07:31, `KeyguardService waited 20002ms, Load: 13.6`, before Field Log was even installed) sat over the screen for the whole job, so F01/F02 died on it, the three tours "passed" by doing nothing, and every fresh Field Log crawler shot has the dialog in the middle. The Field Log dark/200%/landscape crawler PNGs on `latest/` are still the first run's (status bar clock 1:53–1:55; the variants test failed with `EACCES` before writing anything).

Verdict: House Manual: onboarding, the editor and the blueprint are solid on device in light, dark and 200%, and the two fixes since last time (room page names its floor and has an empty state; "Room" title) are confirmed in pictures. But the walkthrough still dies on step two for a product reason, not a flow reason: "Add room" drops every room on the same square, so the second room lands on top of the first, both go red, and the blueprint prints "Living room" over "Kitchen"; a homeowner drawing six rooms would be dragging six stacked boxes apart. The More tab is a Sign in form painted under the status bar, and sign-in itself cannot succeed on the suite because the local backend has no tables. / Field Log: nothing at this sha was seen without an ANR dialog over it, so F1 and F2 are untested for the third run in a row; the one thing the pictures do show is that the Sign in gate, Welcome and Company screens survive a monkey and a release build. Until the suite hides error dialogs and applies the D1 migrations, Field Log's status cannot move.

- [x] [Major] Suite: the local backend has no tables, so every sign-in flow fails with "Couldn't sign in. Try again in a minute." qa.yml starts `wrangler dev --port 8787 --local` and polls `/health`, but never runs `wrangler d1 migrations apply homerecord --local` (vitest does it in `backend/test/setup.ts`; the device suite does not), so `/auth/email/start` throws on `INSERT INTO email_codes` and Hono's `onError` returns 500. The app maps an `HttpException` to `AuthResult.Failed` (that string) and an `IOException` to `Offline` ("You're offline"), so the error on screen proves the backend answered with an HTTP error, not that it was unreachable [judge:qa-d1-migrations] [judge: flow error] - steps: `latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/.../H15-sign-in/screenshots/step-021-assertCondition-hm_sign_in_code.png` (valid email, red "Couldn't sign in. Try again in a minute."); `field-log-after-monkey.png` shows the same text; `.github/workflows/qa.yml` "Start the backend locally" has no migrations step - expected: AC-B1 / walkthrough H17, F1: Send code produces the Code field with the dev backend; qa/README.md says the suite runs against `wrangler dev` - actual: 500 from the backend, `[auto:flow:house-manual:H15-sign-in]` open for three runs. Fix in qa.yml (both jobs): `npx wrangler d1 migrations apply homerecord --local` before `wrangler dev`, publish `qa-out/wrangler-*.log` ([judge:qa-wrangler-log] still open) and record `backend:<app>` as a stage - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/screenshots/flow-H15-sign-in-step-021.png (published under `maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H15-sign-in/`, see [judge:qa-flow-shots-path])
- [x] [UI] Major: House Manual's More tab is the Sign in form drawn under the status bar: "Sign in" sits at y=63 px inside the 136 px status bar, the clock over the S, in light, dark and 200% [judge:hm-more-status-bar] - steps: fresh install → Skip → More; `tour-light-more.png` in every tour folder; H15 failure shot - expected: ui-standards rule 28 (insets from Scaffold, nothing under the status bar), rule 2 (a root may use a top bar); AC-U9 - actual: `house-manual/.../AppTabs.kt` line 107 composes `AccountRoute(container.auth)` bare as the More root; Field Log wraps the same `AccountRoute` in `Scaffold { ... Modifier.padding(it) }` (`field-log/.../AppTabs.kt` line 60) and is fine. Same fix as [judge:fl-roots-status-bar-overlap]. Judged the results the suite published for ca2f492 (run of 2026-10-21 21:16 UTC, 57 stages, 31 passed). `build` HEAD is five commits ahead (4f19dd7 auto entry, 7eed253 Field Log lazy sync on cold start, 5fc429c House blueprint full height in landscape, bdc71e6 debug SEED receivers, f2fd95e fixtures); the suite has not tested HEAD. First run with no ANR dialog and no stale sdcard: every Field Log picture is fresh (35 crawler PNGs incl. Today and More for the first time), the House Manual tour ran in real dark mode and real landscape (navigation rail, 2400×1080), and three fixes from the last entry are confirmed on device: rooms no longer stack ([judge:hm-plan-add-room-stacks]: Kitchen 14' and Living room 12' side by side on the blueprint with dimensions on every edge), the More tab sits under a Scaffold ([judge:hm-more-status-bar]), and Skip keeps Upper/Main/Basement ([judge:hm-skip-drops-floors]). Sign-in is still 500 because this run laid out its jobs from the `main` copy of qa.yml dated 2026-10-20 20:57 (3f2878b, no migrations step); the sync that carries 75004f6 landed on `main` as 894a464 at 20:47 UTC inside this run, so the next run is the first that can exercise [judge:qa-d1-migrations].

Verdict: House Manual: the plan editor and blueprint finally work end to end in pictures, and a homeowner could draw a house with them. But a fresh Two-story + Basement home opens on the Basement, in the House tab and in the editor, so the first six rooms of the walkthrough end up in the cellar unless the user notices the chip; and nothing on the blueprint is reachable by name (one accessibility node for the whole floor), which is why H02, H02b and H03 have been red for five runs even though the screens are right. / Field Log: seen for the first time without a dialog in the way, and it reads as a crew app (roles, invite code, hourly rates, "your boss"). Today is still a lone sync chip over an empty state, and nobody can get past Sign in on the suite until the migrations step actually runs, so F1 and F2 stay untested.

## QA 2026-10-22 (commit 4734e70)

- **Verdict (House Manual)**: The onboarding, plan editor, and blueprint viewer are responsive and well-designed on device; rooms are now properly exposed as accessibility targets and the blueprint expands across the full viewport in landscape mode. The single biggest gap is that the core homeowner inventory and maintenance workflow—adding appliances, data plate scanning, warranties, and reminders—remains unbuilt scaffolding.
- **Verdict (Field Log)**: Authentication, team management with invite code generation, and company settings are in place, and instrumented tests on API 37 now run without Espresso reflection crashes. The single biggest gap is that contractor daily execution—jobs, time clock punches, photo logs, and daily logs—is completely unbuilt, while backend session token issuance in local dev still fails with HTTP 500 unless an explicit JWT secret is passed.

### Verified fixes since last run

- [x] [Critical] Espresso crashes during test idle synchronization on Android API 37 (`java.lang.NoSuchMethodException: android.hardware.input.InputManager.getInstance []`): fixed by pinning `espresso-core:3.7.0` in both androidTest configurations. All 19 tests in `:house-manual:connectedDebugAndroidTest` pass on `qa_pixel` (API 37).
- [x] [Major] `backend/wrangler.toml` hardcodes `ENVIRONMENT = "production"`, causing dev sign-in email-code flow to fail with 502 Bad Gateway: fixed (`ENVIRONMENT = "dev"`).
- [x] [Major UI] `hm-blueprint-room-semantics`: Blueprint rooms are now accessible semantics button nodes with content description, dimension state description, and click actions.
- [x] [UI rule 6] `hm-blueprint-landscape-tiny`: House tab in landscape now displays header, floor chips, layers, and action buttons in a 320 dp column beside a full-height, full-width (1560x1006 px) blueprint canvas.
- [x] [Product gap] `hm-default-floor-basement`: Default floor opens on the entrance floor (Main) rather than the basement.
- [x] [Product gap] `hm-skip-drops-floors`: "Skip for now" in House Manual onboarding preserves the floors implied by the chosen house type (e.g. Basement, Main, Upper).
- [x] [Major UI] `hm-room-page-parent`: Room screen displays the parent floor and home in subtitle (`Main floor · Maple St`) and empty state `Nothing in this room yet`.
- [x] [Minor] `fl-form-close-no-tag`: `FormScaffold` Close and Save buttons are tagged with `<prefix>_close` and `<prefix>_save`, and crawlers accept either `_back` or `_close`.

### Findings

- [x] [Major] backend: `/auth/email/verify` fails with HTTP 500 (`DataError: Imported HMAC key length (0)...`) under `wrangler dev` because `JWT_SECRET` is not configured in `wrangler.toml` or `.dev.vars` - steps: Start backend with `cd backend && npx wrangler dev`, submit email on Sign in screen, fetch code from `/dev/last-code`, submit code to `/auth/email/verify` - expected: AC-B1: endpoint verifies valid code and returns a signed session JWT token - actual: `env.JWT_SECRET` is undefined, `jose` throws `DataError: Imported HMAC key length (0) must be a non-zero value...`, returning HTTP 500 `{"error":"server_error"}` - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-signin-typed.png - fixed: qa.yml writes backend/.dev.vars with a throwaway JWT_SECRET before wrangler dev; README documents it
- [x] [Major] Field Log: debug `SeedReceiver` fails to seed scenario `regression` on device, returning `result=0` after a 10-second broadcast timeout (PLAN Q005 ticked prematurely) - steps: Run `adb shell am broadcast -a com.kurtz.homerecord.fieldlog.qa.SEED -p com.kurtz.homerecord.fieldlog --es scenario regression` - expected: AC-B2, AC-F53, PLAN Q005: `SeedReceiver` calls `/dev/seed`, signs in as `owner@qa.test` via dev auth, and completes with `result=1` - actual: ActivityManager logs broadcast timeout (`took 10017ms`) and returns `result=0`; `SeedReceiver` coroutine fails or hangs during network/keystore calls without logging an error - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-welcome.png - fixed: 4s call timeouts, failures logged under tag QaSeed (earlier result=0 was the JWT_SECRET 500, fixed in qa.yml)
- [x] [UI] Major: Field Log `ScreenCrawlerTest` fails on `company-form` with "2 clickable nodes under 48 dp" because clickable items at the bottom of the scroll container have clipped touch bounds - steps: Run `./gradlew :field-log:connectedDebugAndroidTest` on `qa_pixel` emulator - expected: docs/ui-standards.md rule 30, AC-U12: `ScreenCrawlerTest` verifies all catalog screens meet minimum touch target standards - actual: `ScreenCrawlerTest.everyCatalogScreenOpensAndMeetsTheBasics` fails with `AssertionError: company-form: 2 clickable nodes under 48 dp` because `touchBoundsInRoot` for elements clipped by the scroll viewport edge is smaller than 48 dp - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-company.png - fixed in build (crawlers judge scroll-clipped nodes by full layout size; WelcomeScreen sits in AppTabs Scaffold)
- [x] [UI] Major: Field Log `WelcomeScreen` lacks `statusBarsPadding`, rendering "Set up your company" directly under the status bar - steps: Open Field Log welcome screen (fresh install or `qa.OPEN_SCREEN` with `welcome-create` / `welcome-join`) - expected: docs/ui-standards.md rule 6 and rule 28: edge-to-edge layout with insets from Scaffold; nothing interactive or title text under status bar (starts below 132 px) - actual: "Set up your company" header starts at Y=63 px, placing the top 69 px directly beneath the 132 px status bar - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-welcome.png - fixed in build (crawlers judge scroll-clipped nodes by full layout size; WelcomeScreen sits in AppTabs Scaffold)

## QA judge 2026-10-22 (commit 5125597)

Judged the results the suite published for 5125597 (run of 2026-10-22 02:14 UTC, 57 stages, 33 passed). `build` HEAD is seven commits ahead (fa35334 blueprint room semantics, 4734e70 FormScaffold close tags, ff59fd5 createHome transaction, 5135034/de1829a R03, 7da768f Antigravity QA entry, 1b9ca3c JWT_SECRET for wrangler dev); the suite has not tested HEAD. Confirmed on device this run: a fresh Two-story + Basement home opens on Main in the House tab, the editor and every tour ([judge:hm-default-floor-basement], H02-house-tab.png); the House tab in landscape puts the controls in a column beside a full-height blueprint with every name and dimension ([judge:hm-blueprint-landscape-tiny], house-blueprint-landscape.png); and the migrations step ran, so H15 and F01 got a Code field and a real 6-digit code from `/dev/last-code` for the first time ([judge:qa-d1-migrations]). They then died on `/auth/email/verify`: "Couldn't sign in. Try again in a minute." after a valid code (step-027-assertCondition-Sign_out.png), which is the HTTP 500 Antigravity traced to the missing `JWT_SECRET` under `wrangler dev` (its entry for 4734e70, fixed in the workflow at 1b9ca3c). So this run's F01, F02 and H15 reds are the same single environment fault and the next run on a synced `main` copy of qa.yml decides; without that sync ([judge:qa-yml-main-lag]) it stays red for another run.

Verdict: House Manual: the first three steps of the demo now hold up in pictures: onboarding, Main by default, two rooms drawn and named, a clean blueprint with 14'/12' on every edge. The biggest gap has not moved: there is still nothing to put in a room (the room page's only action is "Back to the house") and the editor still loses or hides detail the user typed (12'6" came out as 12', the plan sits small in a corner, the inspector clips). / Field Log: the sign-in gate works end to end up to the last call, and behind it the catalog screens read as a crew app. Nobody has yet reached Today on the suite's emulator, so F1 and F2 are still judged from fake state; a tour that "passes" with three blank launch shots is not evidence of anything.

- [ ] [Minor] The H02 flow types `12'6"` for the Kitchen length and the room comes out 12' (the Kitchen and the 12×12 default Living room are the same height on the blueprint, 368×316 px for 14:12; the left edge reads 12') [judge:hm-plan-typed-inches-lost] - steps: H02-draw-plan: Add room, Length → eraseText, inputText `12'6"`, Done; `H02-plan-one-room.png`, `step-057-assertCondition-Kitchen.png` - expected: AC-C4 / ui-standards rule 22 (lengths accept 12'6"); the blueprint reads 12' 6" - actual: either Maestro's `inputText` drops the quote characters on this emulator (`126` → 126 ft, rejected, so `SetSize` never fires and the room keeps its default) or `LengthField` → `commitSize` loses the inches; `LengthParserTest` proves the parser, not the field. Decide it in the flow: after the inputText, `assertVisible: "12' 6\""` on the inspector (the field re-formats) and, if Maestro cannot type quotes, type `12.5` (also accepted per rule 22) so the blueprint can be asserted at `12' 6"` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02-draw-plan/2026-10-22_015241/H02-draw-plan/takeScreenshot/H02-plan-one-room.png
- [x] [UI] Minor: the House tab's empty floor shows "Edit plan" twice: the empty state's button in the middle of the screen and the "Edit plan" action in the bottom row, 500 px apart, same label, same destination (`H02-house-tab.png`, every `tour-light-house.png`, `house-empty-floor-*`) [judge:hm-house-empty-double-edit] - steps: fresh home → House - expected: ui-standards rule 10 (one primary action in the empty state that matches the screen's create action) and rule 13 (one primary create control); the empty state's sentence can point at the bottom row, or the bottom row hides while the floor is empty - actual: as above - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02-draw-plan/2026-10-22_015241/H02-draw-plan/takeScreenshot/H02-house-tab.png - fixed in HEAD (empty floor hides the bottom Edit plan; empty-state button carries hm_house_edit_plan)
- [ ] [Minor] Suite: all three Field Log tour shots are blank. `tour-light-launch.png` in `out-tour-dark` is the dark window background with a status bar, in `out-tour-font200` and `out-tour-landscape` the light one; `_tour.yaml` screenshots right after `launchApp` and the app takes 4–5 s to draw its first frame on this emulator (coldstart 4234 ms), so the tour "passed" in 9 s having seen nothing, and `variant:dark|font200|landscape` for field-log count as passes [judge:qa-fl-tour-blank] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/tour-dark.log` ("[Passed] _tour (9s)"); the three PNGs - expected: QA rule 4 (flows deterministic, `extendedWaitUntil`); the launch shot shows the Sign in gate or Today - actual: as above. Add `extendedWaitUntil: visible: "Sign in"` (or `Today`) with a 15 s timeout before the first `takeScreenshot` in both `_tour.yaml`s; do not tick `[auto:variant:field-log:*]` on this run - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-tour-dark/2026-10-22_014953/_tour/takeScreenshot/tour-light-launch.png
- [ ] [Minor] Suite: the house-manual job's crawler `*-dark.png` are all light. Every one of the 27 `house-manual/crawler/house-manual/<screen>-dark.png` is the light theme (status bar at 1:48–1:49, same as the light shots), while the handoff job's copies of the same files (2:09) are really dark and Field Log's dark shots are dark in both jobs, so the dark pass of `ScreenCrawlerTest.darkFontAndLandscapeVariants` ran on the HM job before (or without) the uimode change taking effect [judge:qa-crawler-hm-dark-light] - steps: compare `house-manual/crawler/house-manual/house-blueprint-dark.png` with `handoff/crawler/house-manual/house-blueprint-dark.png` - expected: Q002 acceptance (AC-U8: dark captured per screen) - actual: as above; dark-mode judgments for House Manual this run come from the handoff copies only. Have the crawler assert `isSystemInDarkTheme()` (or the configuration's uiMode) before taking a dark shot and fail the variant otherwise - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual/house-blueprint-dark.png

Still open and seen again this run: [judge:hm-plan-editor-fit] (the catalog plan sits in the top-left quarter of the canvas; H02's second room runs off the right edge), [judge:hm-plan-inspector-clipped] (also at 200% with a plain room: Done/Edit shape under the gesture area, `plan-selected-font200.png`; in landscape the panel covers all but one row of the plan), [judge:hm-blueprint-small-room-name] (half bath unnamed at 100%, named in the editor and in landscape), [judge:hm-blueprint-names-font200] (only Kitchen at 200%; the rotated 6' labels collide with the mudroom outline), [judge:hm-plan-name-over-stairs], [judge:hm-floor-dialog-label-200], [judge:hm-layer-chips-no-icons], [judge:fl-catalog-welcome-insets] (title at y 63 px under a 132 px status bar in all six welcome portrait shots; Antigravity filed the same as Major UI rule 6 at 4734e70), [judge:fl-more-background-seam] (owner and crew More, portrait and landscape), [judge:qa-tour-items-chip] (portrait only: the landscape tour's `tour-light-items.png` is the real Items tab), [judge:qa-crawler-blank-shots] (this run: HM `house-empty-floor-light` and `-font200`, handoff `room-page-font200`, `room-page-landscape`, `team-office-landscape`, `today-crew-font200`, FL `team-error-light`; `house-room-menu-font200/-landscape` without the sheet), [judge:qa-crawler-no-root-bar], [judge:qa-flow-shots-path], [judge:qa-wrangler-log] (still no wrangler log in `latest/`, which is why the 500 had to be inferred from the app's error text), [judge:qa-tour-variant] (files still named `tour-light-*` in every variant). ## QA judge 2026-10-25 (commit b4e1db2)

Judged the results the suite published for b4e1db2 (run of 2026-10-25 10:59 UTC, 66 stages, 44 passed). `build` HEAD is four commits ahead (8295f03 Field Log flows tap company fields by id, aede72c/f1770d3 T037c FL-Reports); the suite has not tested HEAD. Three days and 40 builder runs since the last judge entry (5125597): House Manual gained Home, Items, item page, data plate, warranty lookup, contacts, documents and the paywall; Field Log gained jobs, the job house, the clock, Today, crew clock-in and timesheets. Confirmed on device this run: the sign-in gate works end to end for the first time in both apps (Field Log reaches "Set up your company", House Manual reaches "You are signed in." and signs out, `H15-signed-in.png`); the House tab shows one Edit plan on an empty floor ([judge:hm-house-empty-double-edit]); onboarding, plan editor, blueprint, Kitchen room page, Documents, paywall and all four tour variants render with the bottom bar. Nine of the ten red flows this run are the flows' fault, not the apps' (tagged below); the tenth (H05) fails because the contact form is a half-height sheet with Save below the fold, which is also a rule-15 finding.

Verdict: House Manual: the first screen is finally a product (search, Add item, Emergency card, Needs attention, Recent) and the item page is the best screen in either app, but the house model and the items still do not meet: the Kitchen you drew lists "Nothing here yet" after you add a dishwasher to it, and three of Home's four quick actions open the same blank item form. / Field Log: nobody has reached Today on the suite's emulator yet (every F flow dies on a Maestro selector), so the clock, timesheets and crew punch are judged from catalog shots, where they read as a crew app with real rules (far-from-job reasons, audit lines, locked weeks). Nate's first tap as an owner would be "Add a job" on an empty Today, and that button is not there.

- [x] [Product gap] The room page never lists the room's items. `RoomScreen` renders seven static cards whose only text is the empty sentence ("Nothing here yet. Appliances, fixtures and furniture you add will list here."); it takes no items, photos or anything else, and has no Add action, so a homeowner who adds the dishwasher to the Kitchen (T021, T027a "by room" grouping both work) and taps the Kitchen on the blueprint sees an empty room with no way in [judge:hm-room-items-not-listed] - steps: seed (Items tab shows Dishwasher · Kitchen) → House → tap Kitchen; or `H03-room-kitchen.png` after H04 - expected: PRODUCT.md step 3 "Tap a room and you are in that room: its items, photos, walls"; H5 "room preselected when added from a room"; AC-H10 - actual: `house-model/.../RoomScreen.kt` lines 84-86 and 104-112 (`RoomSections` with `empty` strings only), catalog `room-page-light.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H03-blueprint-room/2026-10-25_102357/H03-blueprint-room/takeScreenshot/H03-room-kitchen.png - fixed in T-room-items (room page lists items, Add an item here preselects the room; warranty status per row still to add)
- [x] [Product gap] Three of Home's four quick actions do not do what they say: "Scan data plate" and the Emergency card's "Add a shutoff" both fire `HomeEvent.AddItem` → the blank Add item form (no type preselected, the data plate button is at the bottom of a 12-field form); "Photograph a wall" only shows the sentence "Wall photos arrive with the room blueprint tools." under the buttons. The hub promises one-tap paths it does not have [judge:hm-home-quick-actions-generic] - steps: Home → Scan data plate / Add a shutoff / Photograph a wall - expected: PRODUCT.md step 4 (scan fills make/model/serial), step 9/13 (shutoffs are one tap from Home), rule 12 (nothing on a hub is deeper than one tap from its full list); a quick action that is not built yet is hidden, not stubbed - actual: `HomeRoute.kt` line 36 (`HomeEvent.AddItem, HomeEvent.ScanDataPlate -> onAddItem()`), `HomeScreen.kt` line 71 (`onAdd = { onEvent(HomeEvent.AddItem) }`), `strings.xml` `home_wall_soon`; `tour-light-home.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H01-onboarding/2026-10-25_101952/H01-onboarding/takeScreenshot/H01-home-after-onboarding.png
- [x] [Product gap] The owner's first Today says "Add a job or invite your crew" and offers only "Invite crew"; there is no add-job action on the screen that tells you to add a job, and no title or company name above the sync chip, so the first screen after creating a company is a chip and an empty state [judge:fl-today-empty-no-add-job] - steps: create a company → Today (catalog `today-admin-*`) - expected: walkthrough F1 "lands on Today with an empty state pointing to Jobs and Team"; rule 10 (one primary action that matches the create action); rule 12 (hub with quick actions) - actual: `today-admin-dark.png`, `today-admin-landscape.png`: briefcase icon, headline, sentence, Invite crew - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/today-admin-dark.png - fixed: Add a job is the primary action, Invite crew a text button below (company name stays in the headline)

The sheet opens half height, so on the device Save sits below the fold and the user must drag before saving; this is exactly why H05 fails (`hm_contacts_save` not found at step 018) [judge:hm-forms-in-sheets] - steps: More → Contacts → + ; item page → Add service visit / Add consumable - expected: rule 15 (more than two fields = full-screen route with Close and Save in the top app bar; two or fewer may be a sheet - the document form qualifies, these do not) - actual: `ContactsScreen.kt` line 83, `ItemUpkeepSections.kt` lines 142/304 (`ModalBottomSheet`), `step-018-tapOnElement-hm_contacts_save.png` shows the sheet cut at the Email field - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H05-contacts/2026-10-25_102714/H05-contacts/screenshots/step-018-tapOnElement-hm_contacts_save.png - fixed in the next commit

## QA judge 2026-10-25 (commit 5619796)

Judged the results the suite published for 5619796 (run of 2026-10-25 15:54 UTC, 67 stages, 42 passed). `build` HEAD is five commits ahead (4a9ad8a room items, 8f40960 Home quick actions, 79ada0b Today Add a job, 7a176bd full-screen forms, 8525716 references), so the three Product gaps and the UI Major from the last judge entry are fixed in code but not yet on a device; this entry judges the sha the pictures belong to. Between b4e1db2 and this sha only Field Log changed (T037c Reports, the `(required)` label fix, the `extendedWaitUntil` fix, R07); House Manual is byte-for-byte the same app as this morning and its four red flows fail at the same four steps. The news is Field Log: the `fl_welcome_name` fix got F01 through company creation for the first time, so Today, Jobs, Clock, Schedule, More, Team and Company exist as real device shots in dark, 200% and landscape (`out-tour-*/takeScreenshot/`), and the three `variant:field-log:*` stages finally test something (they fail on a flow error, below, not on the app). The other six F flows then die for a reason that is the app being right: each one signs in as `qa-owner@example.test` again, the dev backend still holds QA Electric from F01, and the app restores the company and lands on Today instead of "Set up your company" (AC-F4 working as specified).

Verdict: House Manual: same build Nate saw this morning, so the same first words: the Kitchen you drew says "Nothing here yet" after you add a dishwasher to it and three Home quick actions open one blank form; both are fixed at HEAD and the next run decides. Under More, Contacts and Documents are the last two record lists still drawn as stacks of cards with an "Edit" link, and a room you name "Kitchen" stays "Other". / Field Log: on a real device the gate holds: email code, create QA Electric, relaunch straight to Today, Team shows the code, Company edits with Close and Save. What a solo owner then sees is thin: a Today with only Invite crew (fixed at HEAD), a Clock tab offering to "Clock in 0 people" over seven 0h timesheet rows, and a Schedule tab that is still a scaffold placeholder with a developer button.

- [ ] [Minor] Suite: F02–F07 cannot start because they reuse F01's account. Every Field Log flow runs `launchApp: clearState` then `_sign-in.yaml` with `EMAIL: qa-owner@example.test` and waits for "Set up your company"; F01 already created QA Electric for that address on the dev backend, nothing calls `POST /dev/reset` between flows (`device-suite.sh` never does; Q005a built it), so the app correctly restores the owner's company and lands on Today, and step 017 of F02, F03, F04, F05, F06 and F07 fails identically ("Set up your company" not visible, `No jobs yet at QA Electric` on screen). This is the app passing AC-F4, not failing it; `[auto:flow:field-log:F02-invite-join]`, `F03-jobs`, `F04-clock-in`, `F05-today`, `F06-timesheets` and `F07-reports` are all [judge: flow error] [judge:qa-fl-flows-owner-reused] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/F02-invite-join.log`; the step-017 shot in each `out-F0*` folder - expected: QA rule 4 (flows run against a fresh install plus the seed, deterministic): either `device-suite.sh` posts `/dev/reset` before each flow, or each flow signs in with its own address (`qa-owner-f03@example.test`), or `_sign-in.yaml` branches on whichever of "Set up your company" / "Today" appears - actual: six flows assert a screen the app has no reason to show - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-F02-invite-join/2026-10-25_151749/F02-invite-join/screenshots/step-017-assertCondition-Set_up_your_company.png

## QA judge 2026-10-26 (commit 4175a0b)

Judged the results the suite published for 4175a0b (run of 2026-10-25 20:43 UTC, 67 stages, 42 passed). `build` HEAD (d722d8d) is 16 commits ahead (catalog form scaffold, per-flow Field Log accounts, T038b, T039a/b capture); the suite has not tested HEAD, so this entry judges the sha the pictures belong to. This is the first device run of the four REVIEW fixes from the last judge entry, and three of them hold on the emulator: Home shows Add item, Scan data plate and an Emergency card with Add a shutoff (`H01-home-after-onboarding.png`), the Kitchen page has an Items section with "Add an item here" (`H03-room-kitchen.png`), and an owner's empty Today leads with Add a job (`F01-today-empty.png`). The fourth (full-screen contact form) renders right, New contact with Close and Save in the bar, but H05 still cannot press Save, and the screen hierarchy shows why: nothing inside a dialog or bottom-sheet window has a resource id (first finding below). Field Log flows F02–F07 still reuse F01's owner on this sha (fixed in flows at HEAD), so Field Log on device is again only empty screens.

Verdict: House Manual: Nate would draw two rooms, tap Kitchen and finally see an Items section with a way to add one; the record now hangs together, though the room page is still six grey cards and Home still invites him to "photograph a wall" that it no longer offers. The biggest gap is that Add item and New contact have never been completed on a device, because every sheet and full-screen form is invisible to the suite. / Field Log: the solo owner now gets "Add a job" on Today instead of a dead end, but everything else he can see is still empty: no job, punch or timesheet has been created on a real device yet, and Schedule is a placeholder with a developer button. Until F02–F07 run on HEAD, this is a sign-in and company-setup app as far as the device is concerned.

- [x]] [Major] Test tags inside dialog and bottom-sheet windows are not exposed as resource ids. `testTagsAsResourceId = true` is set only on the activity root (`MainActivity` Box); `Dialog` (`FormScreenDialog` in `house-manual/.../FormDialog.kt`) and `ModalBottomSheet` (`core-ui` `Sheets.kt`, the item Type sheet) open their own windows, which do not inherit it. In both failure hierarchies the only resource ids are `android:id/content` and system UI: H05 step 018 sees the texts "New contact" and "Save" but no `hm_contacts_save`; H04 step 021 sees "Appliances, Refrigerator, … Dishwasher" but no `hm_item_type_*` at all. Same will hit Field Log's far-from-job sheet, edit-punch dialog and Approve-all dialog as soon as F04/F06 reach them. This reverses the earlier [judge: flow error] call on `[auto:flow:house-manual:H04-add-item]`: the flow also needs a `scrollUntilVisible` for Furnace, but no scroll will find an id the window does not publish. Still present at HEAD (`FormScreenDialog` unchanged; no `testTagsAsResourceId` outside the two activity roots) [judge:hm-window-tags-hidden] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H05-contacts/2026-10-25_201559/H05-contacts/screen-hierarchy/step-018-tapOnElement-hm_contacts_save.json` (grep resource-id) - expected: CLAUDE.md QA rule 3 (stable testTags on every interactive element, reachable by Maestro ids); set `Modifier.semantics { testTagsAsResourceId = true }` on the content root of every Dialog, ModalBottomSheet and Popup (one place in `FormScreenDialog`, the `:core-ui` sheet wrappers, and the Field Log dialogs) - actual: H04 and H05 fail on device on screens that are drawn correctly - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H05-contacts/2026-10-25_201559/H05-contacts/screenshots/step-018-tapOnElement-hm_contacts_save.png - fixed in this run (unverified on device until QA)

## QA 2026-10-26 (commit 7b4dfdf)

- **Verdict (House Manual)**: House Manual now lets Nate draw a floor plan, pick a room, and add an item with serial age auto-decoding (Rheem MMYY verified on device), but the entire experience still feels like disconnected forms rather than a lived-in home record. The biggest gap is that the Reminders tab remains an unfinished developer placeholder button, the room page presents items as unformatted text without category icons, and naming a room 'Kitchen' defaults to 'Other'.
- **Verdict (Field Log)**: Field Log successfully handles authentication, company setup, job creation, full CameraX photo capture with room/phase tagging, and location-flagged clock punches with week timesheet audits. The biggest gap is that the schedule tab remains an unfinished developer placeholder button, and on device the initial jobs FAB lacks accessible semantics (`NAF=true`).

### Verified Builder Fixes on Device
- `[judge:hm-window-tags-hidden]` **Confirmed Fixed**: `Modifier.windowTestTags()` successfully publishes resource IDs inside modal sheets and dialogs. Both `hm_item_type_*` in the type picker sheet and `hm_contacts_save` / `hm_close` in `FormScreenDialog` are now fully exposed and interactive.
- `[judge:hm-home-intro-wall]` **Confirmed Fixed**: Home intro copy no longer refers to photographing a wall.
- `[judge:roomsOnTheBlueprintAreAccessibleButtons]` **Confirmed Fixed**: Rooms on the House blueprint are accessible nodes with clickable button bounds.
- SerialAgeDecoder on-device verified: Rheem `0415123456` decodes to `2015-04-01` with note `"Decoded from serial (Rheem/Ruud MMYY). Change the date to override."`.

### Findings

- [ ] [UI] Minor: Jobs tab `fl_jobs_new` FloatingActionButton fails framework accessibility (`NAF="true"`) due to missing content description on the clickable container - steps: On Field Log Jobs tab, inspect node `fl_jobs_new` via uiautomator dump - expected: docs/ui-standards.md rule 28 / accessibility guidelines (all interactive buttons must have accessible descriptions) - actual: `fl_jobs_new` node has `NAF="true"` with empty `content-desc` - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-today.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: Contacts screen in House Manual shows duplicate "Add contact" buttons when empty - steps: Open House Manual -> More -> Contacts with no contacts saved - expected: Rule 10 (one primary action in empty state: either the empty state button or the FAB, not both simultaneously) - actual: Both `hm_contacts_add` button in the center empty card and `hm_contacts_fab` FAB at bottom right are rendered simultaneously - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/hm-contacts.png
- [ ] [Minor] `fl_clock_status` card on Field Log Clock tab displays elapsed time text with high precision jitter without formatting padding consistency - steps: Clock in on Field Log Clock tab and observe `fl_clock_elapsed` - expected: Consistent time format (e.g. `00:00:01` or standard timer display) - actual: Displays single-digit minutes/seconds as `0:00:01` - screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-clock.png

## QA judge 2026-10-26 (commit f8c8842)

Judged the results the suite published for f8c8842 (run of 2026-10-26 12:13 UTC, 68 stages, 52 passed). `build` HEAD (93cf97c, T040b3a/T040b3 annotate) is two code commits ahead and untested; this entry judges the sha the pictures belong to. First run where the Field Log account fix and the window-tag fix both reach the device: F03–F07 pass end to end (jobs, clock in, break, clock out, timesheets, reports), H05 saves a contact and H15 passes. The four remaining flow failures are three flow errors (F02, F08, H04) and the known plan-editor semantics (H02b).

Verdict: House Manual: Nate would draw two rooms, open Kitchen and add a contact without a hitch, but the record still looks like a settings app: the room page is six grey cards, Contacts is a grey card with "Edit", Reminders is a developer placeholder, and the plan editor still opens with Living room cut off the right edge. The biggest gap is that a full add-item-from-a-room-with-a-data-plate pass (H4) has still never completed on a device. / Field Log: for the first time a job, a punch and a timesheet exist on a real device, so the time-clock half is real; the photo half that this sha is about was never seen past the camera permission dialog. In the catalog the new Photos tab is two-up grey tiles with captions and the Photo page shows no photo at all, so a crew lead would not yet trust it as the job's camera roll.

- [x] [Minor] F08 never reaches the capture screen: the flow taps `"Don't allow"` (straight apostrophe), the Android 16 dialog says "Don’t allow" (U+2019), the optional tap silently misses and `fl_capture_gallery` stays behind the dialog. The open dialog then survives into the three Field Log tour variants: every `tour-light-launch.png` (dark, font200, landscape) is the permission dialog over the launcher, so `variant:*` for field-log "pass" having seen no Field Log screen [judge:qa-fl-f08-apostrophe] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/F08-capture.log`; step-035 and step-036 shots; the three tour launch shots - expected: QA rule 4 (deterministic flows); tap the deny button by id (`com.android.permissioncontroller:id/permission_deny_button`) or by regex `Don.t allow`, and have `_tour.yaml` wait for "Today" or "Sign in" before its first screenshot - actual: dialog still up at step 036; tours blank - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-F08-capture/2026-10-26_115027/F08-capture/screenshots/step-036-assertCondition-fl_capture_gallery.png - fixed in this run (flow-only plus fl_team_code tag)
- [x] [Minor] F02 joins with the word "SCHEDULE": `copyTextFrom: text: "[A-Z0-9]{8}"` does not match the code as displayed ("K6JM F836", nine characters with a space) and instead matches the bottom-bar label "Schedule" (Maestro matches case-insensitively), so the crew account pastes SCHEDULE and gets "That code doesn't match a company"; the app's error is right. The flow also still taps "Back" on Team (`_tour.yaml` was moved to `fl_back`) [judge:qa-fl-f02-copy-code] - steps: F02 step-062 shot; `qa/flows/field-log/F02-invite-join.yaml` lines 34–36 - expected: QA rule 4; copy from the code's own test tag (`copyTextFrom: id: fl_team_code` or whatever the code Text carries) and tap `fl_back` - actual: Join screen with SCHEDULE and the inline error - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-F02-invite-join/2026-10-26_114155/F02-invite-join/screenshots/step-062-assertCondition-Today.png - fixed in this run (flow-only plus fl_team_code tag)
- [x] [Minor] H04 now fails only because Furnace is below the fold of the type sheet: the window-tag fix holds (the step-021 hierarchy has `hm_item_type_refrigerator` … `hm_item_type_dishwasher`), but the sheet opens half height on Appliances and the flow taps `hm_item_type_furnace` without scrolling [judge:qa-h04-type-scroll] - steps: `grep resource-id` in `out-H04-add-item/.../screen-hierarchy/step-021-tapOnElement-hm_item_type_furnace.json` - expected: QA rule 4; `scrollUntilVisible: element: id: hm_item_type_furnace` inside the sheet before the tap (or type into the sheet's search once [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-no-search] lands) - actual: Element not found - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H04-add-item/2026-10-26_114911/H04-add-item/screenshots/step-021-tapOnElement-hm_item_type_furnace.png - fixed in 91a5837
- [x] [UI] Minor: the instrumented failure on both House Manual jobs is real and new: `ScreenCrawlerTest` stops with "item-detail: 2 clickable nodes under 48 dp", so no item-detail crawler shot exists in any variant. Likely candidates are the `AssistChip` links on the item page (Open manual `hm_item_detail_manual`, warranty Source `hm_item_detail_warranty_source`, recall link), which draw 32 dp tall [judge:hm-item-detail-small-targets] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/house-manual/instrumented/house-manual.txt` (line 197) - expected: rule 30 (touch targets ≥ 48 dp) - actual: 2 nodes under 48 dp; item-detail-* missing from `latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual/` - screenshot: house-manual/src/screenshotTestDebug/reference/com/kurtz/homerecord/housemanual/HouseManualScreenshotsKt/ItemDetailScreenshot_Light_fc5b723e_0.png
- [x] [UI] Minor: the Photo page shows no photo. `AsyncImage` in `PhotoDetailScreen` has no placeholder or error fallback, so when the file does not load (the catalog, a photo still downloading, a deleted file) the image collapses to nothing and the screen opens on the Caption field; the builder's reference PNG hides the same gap. "Marked up" then sits alone as a label over the unrelated "Mark as a Before shot" button and reads like its section header [judge:fl-photo-detail-no-image] - steps: catalog `photo-detail` (Photos tab → a tile) - expected: rule 27 (images through Coil with placeholder and error fallback), PRODUCT.md FL 5 (the photo is the point of the screen); a fixed-ratio image box with a placeholder and a broken-image fallback, and "Marked up" as a badge on the photo - actual: `photo-detail-light.png`: title bar, Caption, Room, Phase, Marked up, Mark as a Before shot, no image area - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/photo-detail-light.png - fixed in 19c2112: the photo sits in a fixed 4:3 frame with a loading spinner and a broken-image fallback ("Couldn't open this photo"), "Marked up" is a badge on it, and a photo from another phone shows Downloading / offline / still uploading with Retry
- [x] [UI] Minor: the Photos tab is a two-up grid of 180 dp tiles with gaps of 8 dp and two lines of text under each, so a day of 20 photos is ten tile rows and about five screens; at 200% a day with five photos already runs past one screen [judge:fl-photos-grid-two-up] - steps: catalog `photos-tab` (Job → Photos) - expected: rule 27 (photo grids 3 columns with 2 dp gaps); PRODUCT.md FL 5 / walkthrough F8 (gallery grouped by day); room · phase belongs on the Photo page or as a small overlay, a pair can span two of three columns - actual: `photos-tab-light.png`, `photos-tab-font200.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/photos-tab-light.png - fixed in ee233c2 (3-column grid, room/phase/caption moved to the tile description and the Photo page)
- [ ] [UI] Minor: the two new Field Log empty states miss rule 10: Photos empty is one sentence and a button (no icon, no headline), and the camera-denied view is one paragraph above three buttons with a blank top half (no icon, no headline) [judge:fl-photos-capture-empty-states] - steps: catalog `photos-empty`, `capture-denied` - expected: rule 10 (icon, one-line headline, one sentence, one primary action) - actual: `photos-empty-light.png`, `capture-denied-light.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/photos-empty-light.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: on the device the capture screen keeps the bottom navigation bar right under the shutter (Today, Jobs, Clock, Schedule, More), while the catalog and the builder's reference PNGs show capture without it; with gloves on, a thumb that misses the 80 dp shutter lands on Today or Clock and leaves the camera. The count beside the shutter is a bare "3" with no thumbnail or label [judge:fl-capture-nav-bar] - steps: Jobs → a job → camera icon (F08 step-035) vs catalog `capture-ready` - expected: PRODUCT.md FL 5 ("volume key shutter, gloves on"), rule 30 (≥ 56 dp, shutter ≥ 72 dp; nothing else tappable crowding it); capture as a full-screen route without the bar, and the last shot as a 56 dp thumbnail that opens it - actual: nav bar at the bottom of `step-035`; "3" in `capture-ready-light.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-F08-capture/2026-10-26_115027/F08-capture/screenshots/step-035-tapOnElement-Don't_allow.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: on the House tab, with rooms drawn, the three actions Edit plan, Floors and Export PDF share one row and two of them wrap to two lines at the default font size ("Edit / plan", "Export / PDF"); the buttons are about 120 dp tall for one-word actions [judge:hm-house-actions-wrap] - steps: House tab after drawing two rooms (H02) - expected: rule 25 (labels from `labelLarge`, single line) and rule 32 (rows may grow at 200%, not at 100%); icon buttons in the top bar or a wrapping row - actual: `H02-blueprint-after-edit.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02-draw-plan/2026-10-26_114321/H02-draw-plan/takeScreenshot/H02-blueprint-after-edit.png
