## Builder code review 2026-10-26 (commit 014c8c3)

CR1: full read of `:core`, `:core-ui`, `:house-model`, `:house-manual`, `:field-log`, `backend/`, `qa/`, `.github/workflows` and the build files against SPEC.md, PRODUCT.md, docs/ui-standards.md, docs/decisions.md and CLAUDE.md. Nothing was fixed in this run. The baseline is green: assembleDebug and testDebugUnitTest pass (610 JVM tests), backend `npm test` passes 77 of 77. The Critical findings were each re-checked against the code. Counts: 11 Critical, 50 Major, 55 Minor. The CR2 subtasks in PLAN.md group these into one-run chunks.

### Critical

- [x] [Critical] Sync unions every `*Json` field, so position lists get corrupted: moving a room merges old and new corners into one polygon, and removed lines, phases and tags come back [cr:sync-json-union] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/sync/RowMerge.kt:24, backend/src/sync/merge.ts:76 - expected: AC-F53 (union only for list-like fields such as photos and tasks; last writer wins for every other field) - actual: polygonJson, cornersJson, pointsJson, slotsJson, linesJson, phasesJson and officeEmailsJson are all unioned on both client and server - fix: union only an explicit allowlist of set-like fields shared by RowMerge and merge.ts; every other field is last-writer-wins; add tests for a polygon edit and for removing an item from a list - fixed in 5a3606a
- [x] [Critical] One rejected op stops all sync on that phone for good [cr:sync-poison-op] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/sync/SyncEngine.kt:81 - expected: AC-F52/F53 (everything syncs after reconnect) - actual: the backend answers 400 for the whole batch when one op names an entity the tenant does not allow. FL-House writes `wall`/`opening`/`stair` under the company tenant, which the backend refuses for that tenant. The op stays at the head of the queue, so after one door on a job plan no punch or photo from that phone syncs again - fix: dead-letter the refused op (log it and show it), then continue; never queue entities the tenant cannot accept - fixed in 5a3606a; shown in CR2a2: both apps' sync status names refused changes and lists them
- [x] [Critical] The pull cursor is one value for the whole phone, but serverSeq counts per tenant [cr:sync-global-cursor] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/data/FieldLogEntities.kt:288, SyncEngine.kt:92-99 - expected: AC-F53/AC-B20 (pull applies every server change of the tenant; the backend keeps `sync_counters` per tenant) - actual: `SyncStateEntity(id = 1)` is never reset on sign-out. Switching company, or House Manual syncing a shared home next to its own (AC-H16), silently skips the new tenant's rows - fix: key sync_state and sync_base by tenant kind and id, and clear outbox, base and state when a membership ends - fixed in d2053eb
- [x] [Critical] Crew, Lead and Sub tokens can read and write every row in the company through sync: approve timesheets, edit other members' punches, change `member` and `company` rows, and pull everyone's punches and rates [cr:sync-role-gate] - where: backend/src/routes/sync.ts:45-58 (push), backend/src/routes/sync.ts:122-131 (pull) - expected: AC-B3 (a Crew token cannot read other members' punches or approve timesheets) and AC-F3 - actual: the only role check is the authoritative pin on punches edited by Owner or Office - fix: add a per-entity, per-role policy table to push and pull (crew may write only their own punches, never approvals, members or company) and add the AC-B3 vitest cases - fixed in 729aab3
- [x] [Critical] The email-code limit can be bypassed: each `/auth/email/start` resets attempts to 0 and nothing rate-limits start or verify, so codes can be guessed without end and someone can flood a mailbox with codes [cr:email-code-bruteforce] - where: backend/src/routes/auth.ts:28-38, backend/src/auth/emailCode.ts:20-27 - expected: AC-B1 (a lockout that actually holds) - actual: unlimited cycles of 5 guesses against 1e6 codes, and unlimited Resend sends - fix: per-email and per-IP KV rate limits on start and verify, and a cumulative failure counter that a new start does not reset; add vitest cases for both - fixed in 9bdd0f1 (per email and per IP limits on start and verify in D1 `auth_limits`, a check counter a new code does not reset; vitest AC-B1 rate limits; off only when ENVIRONMENT=dev)
- [x] [Critical] The default deploy runs with ENVIRONMENT=dev, which exposes `/dev/last-code` (read anyone's sign-in code), `/dev/reset` (wipe every table) and `/dev/seed` [cr:dev-env-default] - where: backend/wrangler.toml:24, backend/package.json `deploy`, backend/README.md "Deploy" - expected: Q005 (/dev/* only in a dev environment); this must fail safe - actual: production is safe only if whoever deploys remembers `--var ENVIRONMENT:production` - fix: default `ENVIRONMENT = "production"` with an `[env.dev]` block (or `.dev.vars`) for local runs and CI, and an explicit production env in the deploy script - fixed in 8e30568
- [x] [Critical] The orphan cron will delete every Field Log photo, voice memo and other non-job_file upload 30 days after it is uploaded [cr:media-orphan-false-positive] - where: backend/src/routes/media.ts:66, core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/photo/JobPhotos.kt:68 - expected: AC-B21 (only unreferenced objects are removed) - actual: an object counts as referenced only if some row's JSON contains its full key. Only `job_file.r2Key` stores the key, so once R2 secrets are set every photo looks orphaned - fix: parse tenant, entity and entityId from the key and keep the object while that live sync row exists; add a test with a photo row - fixed in 0da1365 (cron parses tenant/entity/id from the key and keeps the object while that sync row exists without deletedAt; vitest cases for a photo and a voice memo with no key in their JSON, a soft-deleted row, a missing row and a row in another tenant)
- [x] [Critical] Field Log's location request is ignored on Android 12 and later, so GPS punches never get a fix on current phones [cr:fl-loc-coarse] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/clock/ClockRoute.kt:27-31, core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/location/LocationProvider.kt:27 - expected: AC-F8/AC-F18 (location asked once on first clock-in) - actual: FINE alone is requested through `RequestPermission()`. With targetSdk 37 the system drops that request with no dialog, so it reads as denied. An "Approximate" grant also counts as no permission - fix: `RequestMultiplePermissions(FINE, COARSE)`, accept a coarse grant, and check in a flow that the dialog appears - fixed in 7a8fe68 (RequestMultiplePermissions with FINE and COARSE through `LocationPermissions`, an approximate grant counts as allowed, FusedLocationProvider accepts coarse and asks balanced accuracy then; new flow F04b-location-allow checks the system dialog appears; not run on device)
- [x] [Critical] Leaving FL-Capture within about 3 s of a shot loses the photo, and leaving while recording loses the voice memo [cr:fl-capture-save-cancel] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/capture/CaptureViewModel.kt:180-199, :164 (onCleared) - expected: AC-F24/AC-F27 (every shot and memo is saved to the job) - actual: `save()` waits up to 3 s for a GPS fix in viewModelScope before writing the row. Back cancels it and leaves an orphan JPEG, and onCleared throws away a memo that is still recording - fix: write the row at once with no fix and add the fix later in an app-scoped JobPhotos call; save the memo that is recording in onCleared - fixed in 7a8fe68 (photo rows written at once with no fix in the app-scoped coroutine, fix added after by one UPDATE that skips rows already located; memos and notes save in the same scope; leaving while recording stops and saves the memo; CaptureViewModelTest +2, JobPhotosTest +1)
- [x] [Critical] Field Log sign-out leaves the whole company on the phone: jobs, clients, punches with GPS, pay rates, photos, files, the outbox and prefs [cr:fl-signout-wipe] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/AppContainer.kt:56 - expected: AC-F4 (sign-out clears cached company data after confirmation) - actual: the only listener is `CompanyRepository.clear` - fix: warn about unsynced outbox rows, then clear the Room tables, media and offline dirs and prefs, and refresh the widget - fixed in a0e0059
- [x] [Critical] The data plate sheet crashes when OCR returns the same line twice ("120V", "WARNING" repeat on real labels) [cr:plate-dup-key] - where: house-model/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/housemodel/ui/ItemEditScreen.kt:304, core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/model/DataPlateParser.kt:34 - expected: AC-C31/AC-H5 (scanning a real label works) - actual: `items(scan.lines, key = { it })` throws IllegalArgumentException on a duplicate key - fix: key by index or make the lines distinct in the parser; add a test with duplicate lines - fixed in 2a1ee24

### Major

- [x] [Major] Pull-and-merge can overwrite edits made at the same moment and roll back the other device's edits [cr:sync-merge-race] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/sync/SyncEngine.kt:109-115, RowMerge.kt:17-31 - expected: AC-F53 (field-level last writer wins) - actual: apply() reads and writes with no transaction. With a pending op, every local field that differs from the server is kept, including fields this device never touched; base then becomes the server row and the next push sends the stale values - fix: run apply in `withTransaction` and keep local values only for fields that differ from the old base; add a test for an edit during sync - fixed in d2053eb
- [x] [Major] Device-local columns sync as shared data, and photos and memos have no R2 key [cr:sync-local-columns] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/data/FieldLogEntities.kt:142,149 (`job_file.path`, `offline`), HouseEntities.kt PhotoEntity `path`/`thumbPath`, NoteEntity `voicePath`, core/.../photo/JobFileTransfers.kt:63 - expected: AC-F20 (Available offline is per phone), AC-F55 (photo appears on the other phone) - actual: phone B's cache path and offline flag overwrite phone A's own path; a pulled photo points to a file the other phone does not have and cannot download - fix: move path, thumbPath, voicePath and offline to a local-only table (or exclude them in RoomRowStore) and add `r2Key` to photo and note, set on upload - fixed in bcb9eb4 (path, *Path and job_file.offline never pushed and ignored on pull; r2Key on photo and note set on upload; downloading a pulled photo or memo by its key is CR2b3)
- [x] [Major] Network and file I/O in core run on the caller's thread, and ViewModels call them from Main, so opening a file that is not on the phone crashes with NetworkOnMainThreadException [cr:core-main-io] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/media/MediaUploadApi.kt:62,76, MediaCache.kt:39, photo/JobFileTransfers.kt:57,77, sync/RowStore.kt:28-58 - expected: AC-F19, S7 (no main-thread I/O) - actual: blocking `execute()` inside suspend functions called from viewModelScope - fix: inject an IO dispatcher and wrap these APIs in `withContext(io)` - fixed in b553bf6 (RetrofitMediaTransferApi put/download, MediaCache open/size/trim/freeUp and JobFileTransfers file checks and copies run on an injected IO dispatcher; RoomRowStore queries on IO when called from Main and in place on any other thread, so a repository transaction that records a change still sees its own row; MainThreadIoTest 3)
- [x] [Major] Any exception other than cancellation leaves sync stuck in Syncing and can crash the app on every sync [cr:sync-exceptions] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/sync/SyncEngine.kt:103-106, SyncCoordinator.kt:70-74 - expected: AC-F52 (sync recovers by itself) - actual: a SQLiteException (unknown or missing column when app versions differ) or a SerializationException escapes, kills the collect loop and reaches the uncaught handler - fix: catch Exception and set phase Failed, have RoomRowStore tolerate unknown or missing columns, and add a CoroutineExceptionHandler - fixed in 5a3606a
- [x] [Major] Room schema v1 has been edited in place while testing APKs ship from `build`, so installing over an older APK fails at launch [cr:room-schema-v1] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/data/HomeRecordDatabase.kt:48 - expected: testing APKs install over earlier builds - actual: the identity hash changed at version 1 with no migration and no MigrationTestHelper test - fix: bump the version on every schema change from now on (AutoMigration), commit the exported schemas, and add a migration test - partly fixed in d2053eb (version 2 with AutoMigration from 1, schemas exported as test assets, HomeRecordMigrationTest); bump-every-change discipline stays open under CR2j; fixed in 71702fe (SchemaVersionGuardTest pins the identity hash of each released schema and fails on a change without a version bump; HomeRecordMigrationTest walks every released version to the latest)
- [x] [Major] A markup of a downloaded plan is saved in the evictable download cache, so Free up or trimming loses the new, not-yet-uploaded version [cr:pdfmarkup-cache-loss] - where: core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/photo/PdfMarkup.kt:46 - expected: AC-F21, AC-F20 (Free up drops only downloads) - actual: the destination is next to the source in `cache/downloads/` - fix: write new versions to filesDir/media - fixed in e0deefb (PdfMarkup writes each new version to an injected media dir (filesDir/media), never beside a download; PdfMarkupTest +1)
- [x] [Major] Sync tests miss the cases that break [cr:sync-test-gaps] - where: core/src/test/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/sync/SyncEngineTest.kt - expected: AC-F53 [unit] - actual: no test for polygon or list edits, removing a list item, two tenants in one DB, a 400 on one op, a pull with an unrelated pending field, unknown server columns, or the authoritative punch path on the client - fix: add these with the CR2a/CR2b fixes - partly fixed in 5a3606a (polygon/list edits, removed list item, a refused op, pull with an unrelated pending field, unknown server columns); two tenants in one DB and the client punch path remain for CR2b; two tenants in one DB, forgetTenant and a pull with a pending edit added - fixed in d2053eb (the client punch path is exercised by PunchRepository tests; device-local columns are CR2b2)
- [x] [Major] Concurrent pushes can make pull skip rows forever and lose each other's edits [cr:sync-seq-race] - where: backend/src/routes/sync.ts:75-115 - expected: AC-B20 (monotonic serverSeq), AC-F52/F55 (nothing lost, no duplicates) - actual: read, merge, counter bump and write are separate D1 round trips. If B (seq 6) commits before A (seq 5), a puller passes 5 for good; retried duplicates race into a 500 - fix: one `db.batch` per op with a compare-and-swap on version (or a per-tenant Durable Object), and have pull return only seqs at or below the committed counter - fixed in f5df4ee (each op is one D1 batch that claims the next seq, bumps the counter, logs conflicts and writes the row only while the row is at the server_seq it was merged against; a lost race re-merges on the new row; retried duplicates are acknowledged with their seq; pull capped at the committed counter; sync.test.ts +3 concurrency tests, two fail on the old code)
- [x] [Major] Any member can get a presigned PUT for any key in the tenant and overwrite someone else's object; even the download path mints a PUT URL [cr:presign-overwrite] - where: backend/src/routes/media.ts:33-55, core/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/core/photo/JobFileTransfers.kt:57 - expected: AC-B3 roles, AC-B21 - actual: keys are deterministic from caller input; nothing checks the entity list, row ownership or whether the object already exists - fix: separate presign-put (new object, or the caller owns the row, or Owner/Office) from presign-get, and allow only known entities - fixed in 4754560 (/media/presign signs only a PUT, for a file-carrying entity, under the row's write rule, and only for the key's first uploader or Owner/Office; /media/presign-get signs only a GET for a live row the caller may see; job file downloads use it)
- [x] [Major] AC-B3 has no backend test for a crew pull of others' punches or a crew timesheet approval through sync [cr:test-acb3-sync] - where: backend/test/sync.test.ts:79, backend/test/company.test.ts:91 - expected: AC-B3 [unit] - actual: only the profile, team and invite 404s and the punch pin are tested - fix: add both cases with cr:sync-role-gate - fixed in 729aab3
- [x] [Major] Warranty and data plate calls that succeed upstream but do not parse are not cost-logged, and failed calls still use up the user's quota [cr:lookup-cost-log] - where: backend/src/lookup/warranty.ts:91-97, backend/src/lookup/dataplate.ts:60-67, backend/src/routes/lookup.ts:51,70 - expected: AC-B10 (cost logged per call) - actual: null is returned before the usage - fix: return usage with a null result, log every call, and refund the quota on a 502 - fixed in db6d97d (warranty and data plate return the usage with a null result when the billed answer does not parse; every billed call writes a lookup_log row; the request is given back to the daily quota on 502 and 503; a network error is a 502, not a 500)
- [x] [Major] Company clock rules never reach Crew or Lead and reach admins late: require GPS, punch distance, break rule and overtime rule [cr:fl-settings-source] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/AppContainer.kt:213,233,250, clock/ClockViewModel.kt:71, timesheets/TimesheetsViewModel.kt:58 - expected: AC-F9, AC-F12, AC-F13, AC-F18 - actual: settings come from `companyApi.profile()`, which only admins can read. Crew always get the defaults, and so does everyone offline. The value is read once and never recomputed - fix: send settings with the membership or sync them as a company row, cache them locally, and combine them as a Flow in each VM - fixed in 6b2b73b (/me sends each company membership its clock rules for every role; the app caches them with the membership in PrefsCompanyCache, CompanyRepository.settings is a Flow combined live by Clock, Timesheets and Reports, and Field Log refreshes /me on every return to the foreground)
- [x] [Major] Job status is frozen when the job is saved, so an Upcoming job never becomes Active and never shows in the Clock picker or on Today [cr:fl-job-status-stale] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/jobs/JobDraft.kt:56, clock/ClockViewModel.kt:75, today/TodayMapper.kt:23 - expected: AC-F6, AC-F8 - actual: `statusFor(today)` runs only in JobEdit save - fix: a pure `JobStatusRule.effective(job, today)` with tests (Archived stays archived), used everywhere instead of the stored value - fixed in 5997be4 (JobStatusRule.effective in :core, read for today by Jobs, Overview, Today, Clock and the Timesheets job picker; Archived stays archived, Done with no end date stays done)
- [x] [Major] Role gates exist only on More: Crew can create and edit jobs, add, rename and delete files, and delete photos; a Lead can clock in crew on any job [cr:fl-role-gates] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/jobs/JobsScreen.kt:53, files/FilesScreen.kt, photos/PhotoDetailScreen.kt:65, clock/ClockViewModel.kt:58,75,100 - expected: AC-F3, SPEC §6.1 roles - actual: these screens never see the role - fix: pass CompanyRole (and the lead's job_crew) into each UiState, hide the actions, filter the lead's jobs, and extend RoleGatesTest - fixed in d096e31
- [x] [Major] A widget tap pins Field Log to the Clock tab: every rotation or later relaunch in the same process jumps back to Clock [cr:fl-tab-replay] - where: field-log/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/fieldlog/AppContainer.kt:41, core-ui/src/main/kotlin/com/kurtz/homerecord/coreui/shell/HomeRecordApp.kt:65 - expected: ui-standards rules 5 and 33 - actual: `MutableSharedFlow(replay = 1)` is never reset, and each new composition collects it again - fix: a Channel with `receiveAsFlow()`, or reset the replay cache once the request is handled - fixed in e900f80 (TabRequests in :core-ui hands each request to the shell once through a conflated Channel; a rotation or relaunch keeps the tab the person picked; HomeRecordAppTest +1 fails on the old replaying flow)

### Next session

1. CR2a: fix sync corruption first. Union only an allowlist of `*Json` fields on client and server, dead-letter a refused op, stop sync getting stuck on an exception. Every device is affected today.
2. Quick Critical crash and data-loss fixes: cr:plate-dup-key, cr:fl-loc-coarse, cr:fl-capture-save-cancel.
3. CR2b: per-tenant cursor, transactional apply and local-only columns, before more synced entities land.
4. CR2c/CR2d: backend role policy for sync and auth hardening. These must land before the first real deploy.
5. CR2m: the coverage-check regex and flow renames, so QA results and the gate report real coverage.

## QA judge 2026-10-27 (commit 43f48d0)

Judged the results the suite published for 43f48d0 (run of 2026-10-26 18:27 UTC, 68 stages, 54 passed). `build` HEAD (a21090c, CR2b1) is 11 commits ahead and untested; the newest `## QA auto` entry is also for 43f48d0, so this is the sha the pictures belong to. The three flow fixes from the last judge entry hold for Field Log: F02 now joins a crew account (More shows "QA Electric · Crew") and F08 passes, so all eight Field Log flows are green and the Field Log tours show real screens again. House Manual still has two red flows (H02b, H04) and the crawler still fails on the item page.

Verdict: House Manual: Nate would get through onboarding, a two-room plan and a contact, then add his first item, open the type sheet and find Furnace half-hidden under the gesture bar at the bottom of a half-height sheet, so the one-tap add the app is built around fails on the first try. The record still reads as grey cards (room page, Contacts) with no item ever added on a real device. / Field Log: the time-clock half works end to end on device and a crew member can now join with the code; the new Mark up photo screen draws arrows and "36 in" on a blank page with no photo under them, and Text and Measure are off the right edge of the tool row, so the annotate step a crew lead would show a client does not yet look like marking up a photo.

- [x] [UI] Major: the Type sheet opens at half height with its last visible row under the navigation bar: Furnace is reported at y 2321–2400 on a 2400 px screen, inside the gesture area, so a tap on it (Maestro's here, a thumb on a phone) lands on the system gesture zone, does nothing and drags the sheet to full height instead. This is the app-side half of the H04 failure [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-under-navbar] - steps: Items → Add item → Type; look at the bottom row of the half-height sheet - expected: rule 28 (insets from Scaffold, nothing interactive under the navigation bar); give the sheet content `navigationBarsPadding()` / `WindowInsets.navigationBars` bottom padding, or open it expanded (with [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-no-search] a search field first) - actual: H04 maestro.log "Tapping on element … hm_item_type_furnace … bounds=[0,2321][1080,2400]", then step 023 shows the sheet expanded on Appliances with nothing selected - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H04-add-item/2026-10-26_175540/H04-add-item/screenshots/step-023-tapOnElement-hm_item_room.png - fixed in 91a5837
- [x] [UI] Minor: the item page still fails the crawler's 48 dp check after f967442, now with 1 node instead of 2, so the light crawler run stops at `item-detail` and no `item-detail-*-light` shot (nor any light shot of the screens after it in the catalog) exists; dark, 200% and landscape shots exist. The test does not name the node; the chips fixed in f967442 are now 48 dp in `item-detail-dark.png`, which leaves the Registered `Switch` (`hm_item_detail_registered`) or a section control as the likely one [judge:hm-item-detail-small-targets-2] - steps: `git show origin/qa-results:latest/house-manual/instrumented/house-manual.txt` line 197; `ls latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual | grep light` - expected: rule 30; have ScreenCrawlerTest print the testTag and size of each failing node so the next fix is aimed - actual: "item-detail: 1 clickable nodes under 48 dp" - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/crawler/house-manual/item-detail-dark.png - fixed in 74c12fd (the Registered switch was the one node: 32 dp tall and judged by layout size when the crawler sees it clipped; the whole 56 dp row now toggles; ItemDetailTouchTargetTest reproduces the crawler rule on six item page states and failed on the old code; crawlers now print each failing node tag and size)
- [ ] [UI] Minor: Mark up photo shows no photo. The marks (arrow, circle, "Move panel", "36 in") float on the plain surface, and the empty state says "Drag on the photo to mark it" over a blank page. The builder's reference PNG hides the same gap. It is the same missing image fallback as the Photo page, but here the screen is useless without the photo, because the marks cannot be placed relative to anything [judge:fl-annotate-no-photo] - steps: catalog `annotate`, `annotate-empty` (Photo → pencil) - expected: rule 27 (Coil placeholder and error fallback); PRODUCT.md FL 5 / walkthrough F8 "annotate one"; a fixed-ratio photo box with a placeholder, and the catalog entry pointing at a fixture image (`qa/fixtures/`) so the crawler sees a real photo - actual: `annotate-light.png`, `annotate-empty-light.png`, reference `AnnotateScreenshot_Light` all show no image - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/annotate-light.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: on Mark up photo only Arrow, Circle, Box and Draw fit the tool row at the default font; Text and Measure sit off the right edge with no partial chip or fade to show the row scrolls, and at 200% only Arrow, Circle and Box show. Measure is the tool a contractor reaches for most ("36 in") [judge:fl-annotate-tools-hidden] - steps: catalog `annotate` light and font200 - expected: PRODUCT.md FL 5 (all six tools reachable); a wrapping row, icon-only chips with labels in content descriptions, or a bottom toolbar with all six - actual: Draw is the last full chip at 100%, Box at 200% - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/annotate-font200.png

Field Log in landscape (rail, two panes, rule 1/6) has therefore not been seen on a device. The Field Log `tour-*-launch` shot is also taken before the first frame (`tour-dark` launch is an empty dark surface), because `_tour.yaml` has no `extendedWaitUntil` after `launchApp` as House Manual's does [judge:qa-fl-tour-landscape] - steps: `python3 -c` PNG size check on `latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-tour-landscape/*/_tour/takeScreenshot/*.png` - expected: AC-U8/U9 (one shot per variant), QA rule 4; check whether the app or the flow resets rotation (`launchApp` with a config change, or the FL job's emulator ignoring `user_rotation`), and wait for "Today|Sign in" before the launch shot - actual: portrait shots in the landscape run; blank launch shot - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-tour-landscape/2026-10-26_175935/_tour/takeScreenshot/tour-light-jobs.png

Confirmed on device this run (builder may close): [judge:qa-fl-tour-blank] and [judge:qa-fl-tour-company-close] (the Field Log dark, 200% and landscape tours show Today, Jobs, Clock, Schedule, More, Team and Company, and the tour comes back from Company), [judge:qa-fl-f02-copy-code] and [judge:qa-fl-f08-apostrophe] (F02 and F08 pass). [judge:qa-h04-type-scroll] is not fixed: the flow now scrolls, but `scrollUntilVisible` accepts Furnace at the bottom edge under the nav bar. Add `centerElement: true` there; [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-under-navbar] covers the app side.

Still open and seen again this run: [judge:hm-plan-editor-fit] (`H02-plan-two-rooms.png`: Living room off the right edge; H02b's later shot shows both rooms only after the fit button), [judge:hm-plan-editor-room-semantics] (H02b "Element not found: Kitchen", the room is canvas-drawn), [judge:hm-room-kind-not-inferred] ("14' by 12' · Other"), [judge:hm-contacts-documents-cards] (`H05-contacts.png`: "Maria Soto · Edit" grey card), [judge:hm-house-actions-wrap] ("Edit / plan", "Export / PDF"), [judge:hm-layer-chips-no-icons], [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-no-search], [judge:qa-tour-variant] (all files still `tour-light-*`), [judge:qa-tour-items-chip] (focused Items chip in the 200% House shot), the 200% tab-label wrap ("Remin / ders", "Sched / ule"), [judge:fl-clock-solo-owner-noise] ("Clock in 0 people"), [judge:fl-jobs-rows-no-icon] (Jobs row "Garage subpanel", no client, address or icon), [judge:qa-jank-no-list].

## QA 2026-10-26 (commit a7989b4)

- **Verdict (House Manual)**: The item creation flow is significantly improved now that the Type sheet opens full height with live search, fixing the navigation bar overlap and furnace selection. The single biggest gap remains the lack of behind-the-wall rough-in layers, photo wall texturing, and 3D floor plan visualization (H9–H11).
- **Verdict (Field Log)**: The field capture workflow has taken a massive leap forward tonight: crew can now capture photos, annotate copies with drawing tools, take voice memos with live timers, write field notes, and organize job files into folders. The single biggest gap is that the Daily Log does not yet aggregate these notes, photos, and time entries into a sendable client PDF (F9 / T043).

### Findings

- [x] [Major] Unit test com.kurtz.homerecord.core.data.HomeRecordMigrationTest fails on Windows with IllegalArgumentException: SupportSQLiteDriver expects DB name instead of temp file path - steps: run .\gradlew testDebugUnitTest on Windows - expected: tests pass on all developer platforms (CLAUDE.md: every commit must compile and pass tests) - actual: IllegalArgumentException: This driver is configured to open a database named 'migration-test.db' but 'C:\Users\nkurt\AppData\Local\Temp\robolectric-HomeRecordMigrationTest_...\migration-test.db' was requested. in v1ToV2DropsTheSingleCursorAndKeepsConfirmedRows and v2ToV3AddsR2KeyToPhotoAndNote - screenshot: none - fixed in 8f10736 (Room's name-based MigrationTestHelper compares the text after the last '/', which a Windows path lacks; the test now uses the driver-based helper with AndroidSQLiteDriver and the full database file; not run on Windows here)

### Confirmed fixed

- [judge:hm-item-type-sheet-under-navbar]: Type sheet opens full height above navigation bar, with hm_item_type_search filtering subtypes in real-time ("furn" finds Furnace), populating type and name properly. Screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/hm-item-type-sheet-search.png
- [auto:unit:com.kurtz.homerecord.fieldlog.capture.CaptureViewModelTest.typedNoteSavesToTheJobAndClosesTheDialog]: verified fixed in commit 43f48d0e (141/141 field-log unit tests pass).
- Note & Voice capture (T039c2): Note opens dialog and saves note to job day; Voice requests audio, records with live timer (Stop 0:0x), and saves memo with confirmation. Screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-capture.png
- Photos gallery & Annotate (T040b1, T040b2a2, T040b3): Photos grouped by day, detail viewer with caption/marks/share/delete, and Annotate saves marked-up copy with badge while preserving original. Screenshots: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-photos-annotated-grid.png, qa/screenshots/latest/fl-photo-detail.png, qa/screenshots/latest/fl-photo-annotate.png
- Files tab & Folders (T041b1): Files tab lists empty state, supports search, and creating a folder adds folder row to file list. Screenshot: qa/screenshots/latest/fl-files-folder.png

## QA judge 2026-10-27 (commit 58cbfb0)

Judged the results the suite published for 58cbfb0 (run of 2026-10-27 11:36 UTC, 71 stages, 58 passed); the newest `## QA auto` entry is for the same sha (`build` HEAD has only the later CR2f2 backend commit on top). The two app-side fixes since the last judge entry hold on device: H02b now finds and selects "Kitchen" by name and gets to step 079, and H04 picks Furnace from the full-height Type sheet, saves "Furnace / Carrier", opens the item page and the service form, so an item has been saved by a device flow for the first time. Both flows now stop on flow errors, not app errors.

Verdict: House Manual: Nate can finally add a furnace and open its page, but everything around it still reads as a prototype: the room page is six grey cards with kind "Other", Reminders is a placeholder with a developer button, and there is still no shutoff, panel, wall photo or 3D, which is what the app is for. The biggest gap remains that the house model (room → walls → what is behind them) does not exist past a 2D outline. / Field Log: a crew lead can now sign in, join, clock in with GPS, take photos and open the job's plans offline, which is a usable jobsite day; but the Files tab looks like a developer list (three icon buttons on every row, Add file as a button instead of a FAB, a bare sentence for the empty state) and there is still no daily log, so the "log writes itself" promise is the biggest gap.

- [x] [Minor] H02b flow error: Delete stairs now opens the "Delete these stairs?" confirm (rule 16, correct since ae49139) and the flow taps "Back" while the dialog is up, so it fails "Element not found: Back" [judge:qa-h02b-delete-stairs-confirm] [judge: flow error] - steps: `qa/flows/house-manual/H02b-shape-doors-stairs.yaml` line 21-22 - expected: QA rule 6; tap "Delete" in the dialog (and assert "Add stairs" is back) before `tapOn: "Back"` - actual: step 079 shows the confirm dialog over the plan - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02b-shape-doors-stairs/2026-10-27_110739/H02b-shape-doors-stairs/screenshots/step-079-tapOnElement-Back.png - fixed in 1323b69 (flow confirms through hm_plan_delete_confirm and checks the stairs are gone)
- [x] [Minor] H04 flow error: the flow opens Add service visit (a full-screen form, rule 15, correct) and then scrolls for `hm_item_detail_document_add` without closing it, so it fails "No visible element found" on the form [judge:qa-h04-service-form-close] [judge: flow error] - steps: `qa/flows/house-manual/H04-add-item.yaml` lines 98-105 - expected: QA rule 6; tap `hm_close` (or fill and Save the visit and assert the row) before scrolling to Documents - actual: step 064 shows the empty Add service visit form - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H04-add-item/2026-10-27_111205/H04-add-item/screenshots/step-064-scrollUntilVisible-hm_item_detail_docume.png - fixed in 3b15b90 (tap hm_close, assert the form is gone, then scroll to Documents)
- [x] [UI] Minor: forms opened through `FormDialog` (Add service visit seen on device) draw white status-bar icons on the light surface, so the clock, signal and battery vanish; the item form in the main window has dark icons [judge:hm-form-dialog-status-icons] - steps: Items → an item → Add service visit (light theme) - expected: rule 34 (contrast) and rule 28; set the dialog window's `isAppearanceLightStatusBars` from the theme (`WindowCompat.getInsetsController(dialogWindow, view)`), or make these forms real routes - actual: "11:14", Wi-Fi and battery white on #F8F8FF - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H04-add-item/2026-10-27_111205/H04-add-item/screenshots/step-064-scrollUntilVisible-hm_item_detail_docume.png - fixed in 3b15b90 (FormScreenDialog sets the dialog window's status and navigation bar icon appearance from the form surface's luminance)
- [ ] [UI] Minor: on Add item with the keyboard up, the scrolling form is clipped about 85 dp above the keyboard: the Model field is cut through its middle and a blank band sits between it and the suggestion strip, which looks like the IME inset applied twice (Scaffold insets plus `imePadding()`) [judge:hm-item-form-ime-gap] - steps: Items → Add item → pick a type → tap Make and type - expected: rule 23 (focused field and the next one visible right above the keyboard, no dead band) - actual: Model cut at y≈1285 px, keyboard top at y≈1510 px - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H04-add-item/2026-10-27_111205/H04-add-item/takeScreenshot/H04-item-form.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: every Files row carries three trailing icon buttons (Available offline, rename pencil, delete bin); at 200% they squeeze the name to one word per line ("Ortiz first / floor plan.pdf / 18.4 MB · / Offline"), and a bin on every row invites accidental deletes on a jobsite [judge:fl-files-row-actions] - steps: Jobs → a job → Files (catalog `files-tab`) - expected: rule 7 (one trailing meta or chevron), rule 18 (long-press selection for actions); keep the offline state as a trailing icon or supporting text, move rename/move/delete to an overflow or long-press - actual: `files-tab-light.png`, `files-tab-font200.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/files-tab-font200.png
- [ ] [UI] Minor: Files empty is one sentence under a filled "Add file" button and an outlined "New folder" button; no icon, no headline, and the create action is a top-of-list button rather than the FAB (the builder's reference PNG shows the same) [judge:fl-files-empty-and-add] - steps: a job with no files → Files (catalog `files-empty`) - expected: rule 10 (icon, headline, one sentence, one action matching the FAB) and rule 13 (primary create = FAB, or a FAB menu for Add file / New folder / Scan) - actual: "No files on this job yet. ## QA judge 2026-10-28 (commit 03cfcee)

Judged the results the suite published for 03cfcee (run of 2026-10-27 22:22 UTC, 71 stages, 62 passed); the newest `## QA auto` entry is for the same sha (`build` HEAD d15eb01 adds only CR2l1 and the lookup-cost fix on top). This is the first run where every House Manual and Field Log flow passes end to end (21/21); what is left red is cold start and launch-frame jank. The commits since 58cbfb0 are sync, media and test fixes plus two flow fixes; the only visible app change is the item page's Registered row, and the crawler now produces `item-detail-*-light` shots.

Verdict: House Manual: Nate can name the house, draw two rooms, add a furnace with make and model and find it in the Kitchen, and all of it now runs clean on a device; but he will open the Kitchen and see six grey cards saying "nothing yet" with kind "Other", and Reminders is still a placeholder with a developer button. The biggest gap is unchanged: no walls, photos, shutoffs, panel or 3D, so the house model stops at a 2D outline and the app does not yet do what it is for. / Field Log: a crew can sign in, join with a code, clock in with GPS, take photos, open plans offline and the owner can approve timesheets, which is a real jobsite day; but Today has no Clock in button and no tasks, Schedule is a placeholder, and there is no daily log. The biggest gap is the daily log (and tasks), which is what turns this from a time clock into a jobsite app.

- [ ] [UI] Minor: in Edit shape the selection panel covers the bottom half of the canvas and the selected room is not panned into view: Kitchen sits at the bottom edge with only its top two corner handles visible, while the hint above says "Drag a corner to move it" [judge:hm-plan-shape-corners-hidden] - steps: House → Edit plan → tap Kitchen → Edit shape (portrait, 100% font; H02b) - expected: walkthrough H2 (drag, resize, L-shape) and rule 23 spirit; when a room is selected, pan or fit so the whole room sits in the visible canvas above the panel (or collapse the panel to a sheet in shape mode) - actual: room clipped at the panel's top edge, two of four handles reachable (landscape variant is in [judge:hm-plan-editor-fit]) - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02b-shape-doors-stairs/2026-10-27_214916/H02b-shape-doors-stairs/takeScreenshot/H02b-edit-shape.png
- [ ] [Minor] Suite/catalog: the job sub-tab catalog entries render without the job's top bar and draw under the status bar: Files' search field runs through the "9:38" clock and Photos' "Wednesday, October 21, 2026" header sits behind the status icons, so the crawler cannot check rule 28 on these screens [judge:qa-crawler-job-tab-insets] - steps: catalog `files-tab`, `photos-tab` - expected: rule 28; render tab entries inside the job scaffold (title, tabs) as in the app, or apply the status-bar inset when shown alone; if the app itself shows them this way, it is a Major rule 28 failure - actual: `files-tab-light.png`, `photos-tab-light.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/files-tab-light.png

Confirmed on device this run (builder may close): [judge:hm-item-detail-small-targets-2] (instrumented ScreenCrawlerTest passes on house-manual, 20 tests; `item-detail-light.png` now produced). [judge:hm-item-form-ime-gap] could not be checked: `H04-item-form.png` has Model focused but no keyboard up.

Still open and seen again this run: [judge:hm-plan-editor-fit] (`H02-plan-two-rooms.png`: Living room cut at the right edge), [judge:hm-plan-inspector-clipped] (`H02b-stairs.png`: a third button row cut at the panel bottom), [judge:hm-room-kind-not-inferred] (`H03-room-kitchen.png`: "14' by 12' · Other"), [judge:hm-room-item-rows], [judge:hm-contacts-documents-cards] (`H05-contacts.png`), [judge:hm-documents-three-adds] (`H06-documents.png`: Scan a document, Add document and FAB), [judge:hm-house-actions-wrap] ("Edit / plan", "Export / PDF"), [judge:hm-layer-chips-no-icons], [judge:hm-reminders-placeholder-button], [judge:hm-item-hero-icon-too-tall], [judge:hm-more-signed-in-no-account] (`H15-signed-in.png`), [judge:qa-hm-tour-landscape] and [judge:qa-fl-tour-landscape] (all 15 landscape shots 1080×2400), [judge:qa-tour-variant] (every variant still `tour-light-*`), [judge:qa-fl-tour-blank] (`tour-light-launch` blank in dark and 200%), the 200% tab-label wrap ("Remin / ders", "Sched / ule"), [judge:fl-clock-solo-owner-noise] ("Clock in 0 people"), [judge:fl-clock-in-64dp], [judge:fl-today-invite-crew-orphan], [judge:fl-schedule-placeholder-button], [judge:fl-files-row-actions], [judge:fl-files-empty-and-add], [judge:fl-jobs-rows-no-icon], [judge:fl-photos-capture-empty-states] (`capture-denied-light.png`: blank top half), [judge:qa-jank-no-list].

The biggest gap is still the house model stopping at a 2D outline (no walls, photos, shutoffs, panel or 3D) with a Kitchen page of six grey "nothing yet" cards and a placeholder Reminders tab. / Field Log: the crew day got tighter (Today has a Clock in card, Clock buttons are big enough for gloves, photos sit in a proper 3-up grid), but Nate would still open Schedule and hit "This screen is on its way", and the job has Log and Tasks tabs with nothing behind them. The biggest gap is the daily log and tasks (T043/T044), which is what makes this a jobsite app rather than a time clock with a camera.

## QA judge 2026-10-29 (commit da39b7e)

Judged the results the suite published for da39b7e (run of 2026-10-28 23:00 UTC, 71 stages, 61 passed); the newest `## QA auto` entry is for the same sha and `build` HEAD 5a99fc1 adds only debug-seed bookkeeping (CR2t). Since 7f9672c the visible changes are: LinkChipRow chips at 48 dp (the crawler's light pass now completes for Field Log, 68/68 `-light` shots), room page item rows with an icon and warranty status, a shared PdfViewer with page thumbnails in both apps, discard prompts on House Manual forms, and delete confirms on consumables and service visits. No PLAN task was newly ticked (45, same as last run). All 21 flows pass; only cold start and launch jank are red.

Verdict: House Manual: the record got safer (every delete and discard now asks first) and the manual viewer finally shows real pages, but on a fresh house the Kitchen still reads "14' by 12' · Other" over six "nothing yet" cards and the Reminders tab is still a placeholder. The biggest gap is unchanged: the house stops at a 2D outline, with no walls, photos, shutoffs, panel or 3D behind it. / Field Log: the chip fix means every Field Log screen now renders in light, and the landscape tour finally rotates to a nav rail, which exposes the first thing Nate would hit on a jobsite: turn the phone sideways to shoot a wall and the viewfinder is a 50-pixel black strip. Beyond that the job still has Log and Tasks tabs with nothing behind them and Schedule says "This screen is on its way" (T043/T044).

- [x] [Product gap] FL-Capture in landscape shrinks the camera preview to a thin black strip (about 50 px of a 1080 px tall screen) under the top bar, while the room chips, phase chips, Note/Voice buttons and the shutter row take the rest; a crew member who rotates the phone to frame a wall or a panel cannot see what they are shooting [judge:fl-capture-landscape-viewfinder] - steps: Jobs → a job → Photos → Take a photo, rotate to landscape (catalog `capture-ready`, `capture-ghost`, `capture-note`, `capture-recording` landscape) - expected: PRODUCT.md (photos are the core of the crew's day; the viewfinder leads), rules 30 and 33 (shutter reachable, rotation keeps a working screen); in landscape put the preview full height on the start side and the chips, Note/Voice and the 72 dp shutter in a column on the end side, as the system camera does - actual: `capture-ready-landscape.png` shows the flash and flip icons on a black band at y≈200–250 and the rest of the screen is chips and buttons - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/capture-ready-landscape.png - fixed in e7f5d4a
- [ ] [Minor] The new shared PdfViewer has no page indicator and no landscape layout: in landscape the page is zoomed to full width inside a ~400 px band, so only the title line of page 1 shows ("Garage subpanel plan" at display size, second line cut), upscaled and soft, and the thumbnail strip below shows no current page highlight; in portrait the same strip gives no "1 of 3" either [judge:core-pdf-viewer-landscape] - steps: Field Log Files → Garage plan.pdf, or House Manual Documents → Furnace manual.pdf; rotate (catalog `file-viewer`, `document-viewer`) - expected: rule 33 (rotation keeps a usable screen) and rule 26/31 (the strip reads as a page picker: selected thumbnail marked, "Page 1 of 3" announced); in landscape fit the page to height or put the thumbnails in a side rail - actual: `file-viewer-landscape.png`, `document-viewer-landscape.png` - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/file-viewer-landscape.png

## QA judge 2026-10-29 (commit 82c76a0)

Judged the suite results for 82c76a0 (run of 2026-10-29 12:36 UTC, 62 stages, 50 passed); `build` HEAD 23a266c is that run's QA auto commit on top of 396bdf4 (CR2b4, crew punch-change history), which the suite has not tested yet. Since da39b7e the visible changes are: FL-Capture in landscape (viewfinder full height on the start side), Contacts and Documents as list rows, item page sections as headers over rows, the House Manual debug seed, the "changes didn't sync" chip and list in both apps, photo and voice-memo download placeholders, and tours named for their variant. No PLAN task was newly ticked (44). Four House Manual flows and F08b fail; the four HM failures have one cause, a step the flow added, and F08b is a real app failure.

Verdict: House Manual: Contacts and Documents finally look like the rest of the app (rows with call and email actions), but the first thing Nate would see after signing in is "Sync failed. Tap to retry" on More, so the backup and second-phone story (H17) is not shown working. The biggest gap is unchanged: the house stops at a 2D outline with no walls, photos, shutoffs, panel or 3D. / Field Log: the capture screen now looks right sideways, but on the emulator the shutter answers "Couldn't save the photo. Check free space and try again." and the job's Photos tab never gets a photo. The crew's core action, taking a job photo, is not shown working anywhere this run: not on device, not in the crawler, not in the builder's own reference PNGs, which all show "Couldn't open this photo" or grey tiles.

- [x] [Major] House Manual More shows "Sync failed. Tap to retry" right after a successful sign-in against the local backend, while Field Log on the same runner and backend shows "Everything is synced"; a new account with an empty house should have nothing to fail on, and H17 is about the record syncing [judge:hm-signin-sync-failed] - steps: fresh install, More → Sign in with email → code → back on More (flow H17-sign-in, which passes because it does not assert the sync status) - expected: PRODUCT.md H17 (sign in, the home appears and edits sync); SPEC AC-H sync status reads "Everything is synced" after a clean sync; if it does fail, the chip says what failed and the log says why (no app log line names the error this run) - actual: "You are signed in." over a "Sync failed. Tap to retry" chip - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H17-sign-in/2026-10-29_122001/H17-sign-in/takeScreenshot/H17-signed-in.png - fixed in 7324c17
- [ ] [UI] Minor: when the shutter fails, FL-Capture prints one line of small red text across the bottom of the live preview ("Couldn't save the photo. Check free space and try again.") with no container, no Retry and no log line with the cause; on the yellow-green emulator scene it is hard to read, and the free-space advice is a guess (F07b on the same device reports plenty of space) [judge:fl-capture-save-error] - steps: Jobs → a job → Photos → Take a photo → shutter (flow F08b) - expected: rule 10 (error says what failed and offers Retry), rule 34 (4.5:1 contrast; put it in a snackbar or a scrim-backed banner, not bare text on the camera image); the message names the actual failure (camera error vs write error) and the exception is logged - actual: red bodySmall text at y≈1680 over the preview - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/maestro/qa-out/device/field-log/maestro/out-F08b-capture-photo/2026-10-29_122729/F08b-capture-photo/screenshots/step-041-assertCondition-1.png
- [x] [UI] Minor: the plan editor's inspector panel does not move above the keyboard: while the room name is focused, the "Kind" button is cut by the keyboard and Done, Edit shape and Delete room are hidden under it, so a person who types a name has to dismiss the keyboard to finish (the IME key is Next/Tab, not Done) [judge:hm-plan-inspector-ime] - steps: House → Edit plan → Add room → tap Room name (or rotate while typing) - expected: rule 21 (IME Done on the last field commits) and rule 23 (insets: the panel takes `imePadding()` and scrolls so its primary action stays reachable) - actual: name field at y≈1100, keyboard from y≈1260, nothing of the panel's actions visible - screenshot: qa-results:latest/house-manual/maestro/qa-out/device/house-manual/maestro/out-H02-draw-plan/2026-10-29_121258/H02-draw-plan/screenshots/step-058-tapOnElement-Done.png - fixed in 332fdb2: the inspector fields scroll under a pinned Done row; a tapped wall or door shows only its own controls (Done goes back to the room); on a window ≥ 600 dp wide and wider than tall the panel sits beside the plan; the panel is padded above the keyboard
- [ ] [Minor] Suite: no Field Log catalog entry or screenshot test shows a photo. `photo-detail` (the normal state) renders "Couldn't open this photo" in the crawler and in the builder's own `PhotoDetailScreenshot_Light` reference, `photos-tab` is grey tiles, `annotate` draws its marks on a blank page and `capture-ghost` has no Before image; so [judge:fl-photo-detail-no-image] was ticked without anyone seeing FL-Photo with a picture, and with F08b failing on device the photo screens are unverified end to end [judge:qa-fl-photo-catalog-no-image] - steps: catalog `photo-detail`, `photos-tab`, `annotate`, `capture-ghost`; compare `field-log/src/screenshotTestDebug/reference/.../PhotoDetailScreenshot_Light_fc5b723e_0.png` - expected: QA rule 3 (realistic fake state) and Q005 (fixtures): point the fake photo paths at a bundled fixture image (`qa/fixtures/wall-kitchen.jpg` copied into test assets, or a generated bitmap) so the screens show what a crew sees - actual: error placeholder in the happy-path entry - screenshot: qa-results:latest/field-log/crawler/field-log/photo-detail-light.png
- [ ] [Minor] Suite: the jank stage no longer runs: both apps record `jank:<app>` skip "not measurable: no benchmark apk", so the fix for [auto:jank:*] (dcbb1c4, benchmark build type and 200-row fling) has never been measured and AC-U11's 200-row scroll is untested; qa.yml copies `$app/build/outputs/apk/benchmark/$app-benchmark.apk` and nothing is there (check the output file name, e.g. `-benchmark-unsigned.apk`, and whether `assembleBenchmark` failed under `--continue` in latest/apk/gradle.log) [judge:qa-jank-benchmark-missing] - steps: qa-results latest/summary.md stages `jank:house-manual`, `jank:field-log` - expected: SPEC AC-U11 / ship-ready S7 (200-row lists scroll without jank, measured) - actual: skip in both apps - screenshot: none (stage table)

Auto entry review (6 new): 
- [auto:flow:house-manual:H02-draw-plan] [judge: flow error]: the app did what rule 33 asks: after `setOrientation: PORTRAIT` the room name "Living room" is kept and focused with the keyboard open, and the flow's next `tapOn: "Done"` (H02-draw-plan.yaml line 47) cannot find Done under the keyboard; line 29 of the same flow already needs `hideKeyboard` before its Done. Add `hideKeyboard` after the rotation asserts. The app side of it is filed above as [judge:hm-plan-inspector-ime].
- [auto:flow:house-manual:H02b-shape-doors-stairs], [auto:flow:house-manual:H03-blueprint-room], [auto:flow:house-manual:H04-add-item] [judge: flow error]: each runs H02-draw-plan.yaml first and fails on the same step (step-060 screenshots are identical to H02's); they did not reach their own steps. Fixing H02 fixes these.
- [auto:flow:field-log:F08b-capture-photo]: not a flow error. The shutter fired and the app showed "Couldn't save the photo"; logcat for the run has CameraX binding the back camera (only camera id 1; the front-camera validator warns) and no error line from the app for the failed save. F08b is new since caf64df (CR2m3), so this is the first time the shutter has been pressed on a device by the suite.
- [auto:instrumented:field-log:field-log] [judge: flow error] (test, not app): `FilesAndMediaAcceptanceTest.downloadsAreCachedOnceAndTheCacheKeepsToItsCap` sets a.pdf's mtime to 1000, then opens a.pdf again as a cache hit, and `MediaCache.open` touches the file on a hit (`setLastModified(now)`, line 46), so a.pdf becomes the newest and b.pdf is evicted; the assertion `a.exists() == false` contradicts LRU.
